199. `check_operator_authorize_bypass_requires_session_budget` — UNBLOCK + REVIEW-FIX + RETRY DISPATCH self-protection 2026-05-13 (operator approved). Refuses .py/.sh callers under `tools/`, `scripts/`, `experiments/`, `src/tac/` (excluding `experiments/results/`, `_intake_`, `.omx/oss_export/`, `vendored`, `/tests/`, `/test_` markers + canonical `tools/operator_authorize.py` + `src/tac/preflight.py` self-exempt) that set the env var `OPERATOR_AUTHORIZE_CONFIRMED_VIA_SESSION_DIRECTIVE` without ALSO setting `OPERATOR_AUTHORIZE_SESSION_BUDGET_USD` within a 30-line callsite window. Bug class: the orchestrator failure mode anchored 2026-05-13 (BLOCKED report `feedback_5_parallel_substrate_dispatch_orchestrator_BLOCKED_20260513`) showed `tools/operator_authorize.py::_confirm()` raises EOFError when invoked from `subprocess.run(capture_output=True)` (stdin closed) — `_confirm` returns `default_yes=False` and dispatch is silently aborted with "aborted - no dispatch fired". The fix lands a paired-env-var bypass in `_confirm()`: when BOTH `OPERATOR_AUTHORIZE_CONFIRMED_VIA_SESSION_DIRECTIVE=1` AND `OPERATOR_AUTHORIZE_SESSION_BUDGET_USD=<float>` are set, the prompt is bypassed and a LOUD `[OPERATOR-AUTHORIZE BYPASS ACTIVE]` banner is logged to stderr with the explicit budget cap. Bare CONFIRMED=1 without BUDGET raises `SystemExit(11)` at runtime to prevent partial-config bypass. The paired-budget requirement forces the operator to explicitly acknowledge the session-wide envelope; CONFIRMED-alone would be a blanket auto-approve safety regression. Same-line waiver `# OPERATOR_AUTHORIZE_BYPASS_OK:<reason>` for rare callers that combine the directive with a built-in cap. STRICT-from-byte-one per CLAUDE.md "Bugs must be permanently fixed AND self-protected against" + "Strict-flip atomicity rule"; live count at landing: 0. 18 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_check_198_operator_authorize_bypass.py` covering live-repo regression, positive .py/.sh detection (CONFIRMED without BUDGET), inline-VAR shell form, negative both-vars accepted, comment-only mention not flagged, same-line waiver opt-out, canonical-tool + preflight self-exempt, test files exempt, out-of-window budget rejected, strict-mode-raises, plus 5 runtime subprocess tests for `_session_directive_bypass_active()` helper. Memory: `feedback_unblock_review_fix_retry_dispatch_landed_20260513.md`. Sister of Catalog #143 (`check_paid_job_register_before_submit`) + Catalog #167 (`check_substrate_dispatch_uses_smoke_before_full_pattern`) — together they keep the operator-authorize → smoke-before-full → paid-job chain coherent under non-interactive subprocess invocation.

201. `check_modal_sentinel_files_are_in_mount_set` — PR95++ Modal smoke [redacted-modal-call-id] @ 2026-05-13 21:59:30 UTC returned rc=13 (`MISSING_WORKER`) after `tools/operator_authorize.py::_modal_sentinel_files` appended the recipe YAML path under `.omx/operator_authorize_recipes/<recipe>.yaml` to the `--sentinel-files` list passed to Modal dispatch. Catalog #166's worker-side hash ledger then refused the dispatch because `.omx/` is NOT one of the canonical mount-set prefixes declared by `src/tac/deploy/modal/mount_manifest.py::STRUCTURAL_MINIMUM_DIRS` (`src/`, `scripts/`, `upstream/`, `submissions/`, `experiments/`, `tools/`, or exactly `pyproject.toml`) — so the worker had no matching sentinel to hash and surfaced `MISSING_WORKER`. The dispatch failed BEFORE training started, blocking ALL substrate dispatches through `operator_authorize.py` until the fix landed. Bug class: any sentinel path outside the Modal mount set ALWAYS produces `MISSING_WORKER` and refuses dispatch rc=13. Companion runtime fix in `tools/operator_authorize.py` (1) removes the recipe-YAML `.append` call, (2) adds a module-level `_MODAL_MOUNT_SET_PREFIXES` tuple mirroring `STRUCTURAL_MINIMUM_DIRS`, and (3) adds a runtime filter `_path_under_modal_mount_set(rel)` that drops + WARNs on any raw_paths entry outside the mount set. This STRICT preflight gate is the structural self-protection per CLAUDE.md "Bugs must be permanently fixed AND self-protected against" non-negotiable: it parses `tools/operator_authorize.py::_modal_sentinel_files` via AST and refuses any string-literal sentinel path (in the seed `raw_paths` list literal OR in a `raw_paths.append("<lit>")` call) that is NOT under one of the canonical mount-set prefixes. Dynamic expressions (`recipe.remote_driver`, `str(value)`, `entry.strip()`, etc.) are NOT statically checkable and are silently skipped — only `ast.Constant` string literals are flagged. Same-line waiver `# SENTINEL_OUTSIDE_MOUNT_OK:<reason>` honored on the literal's source line (placeholder `<reason>` literal rejected). Sister of Catalog #166 (`check_modal_dispatch_verifies_worker_source_matches_head`, the worker-side hash check that surfaces the failure rc=13) and Catalog #153 (`check_modal_dispatcher_uses_canonical_mount_builder`, the canonical mount builder that defines `STRUCTURAL_MINIMUM_DIRS`) — together the three gates close the mount-vs-sentinel parity bug class at design (#153) → source (#201) → dispatch (#166). STRICT-from-byte-one per CLAUDE.md "Strict-flip atomicity rule"; live count at landing: 0. 30 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_check_201_modal_sentinel_files_in_mount_set.py` covering positive (`.omx/` / `.ralph/` / `configs/` / `docs/` / `cuda/` / `runtime-rs/` literals), positive (seed-list `ast.Assign` AND `ast.AnnAssign` shapes per Catalog #168), positive (multi-violation seed-list + mixed seed+append), negative (each of the 6 canonical mount-set prefixes accepted), negative (`pyproject.toml` exact-match accepted; `pyproject.toml.bak` substring rejected), negative (dynamic Attribute / Call / method-call expressions skipped), waiver semantics (rationale accepted, `<reason>` placeholder rejected, off-line waiver not honored), strict mode (raises `PreflightError` on violations; silent on clean), missing target file / missing function / syntax-error target all silent, plus live-repo regression guard. Memory: `feedback_catalog_201_modal_sentinel_mount_parity_landed_20260513.md`.

206. `check_subagent_dispatches_use_checkpoint_discipline` — Subagent crash-resume discipline 2026-05-14 (operator directive "why did it die? need to investigate and fix permanently"). Empirical anchor: Wyner-Ziv research subagent (id `a1362a24d986029c3`) crashed mid-session with Anthropic API `Internal server error` after 17 minutes / 58 tool uses / 1704 tokens; all in-flight progress was lost. Sister pattern WAVE-3-HNERV-C-RETRY (DSNeRV + HiNeRV trainers) hit the same failure class but survived because intermediate commits had already landed. Bug class: long-running subagents accumulate non-trivial work in-context (file edits, research synthesis, dispatch plans) that is INVISIBLE to the parent until either (a) a commit lands, or (b) the subagent reports back. When the API crashes mid-session, every uncommitted-and-unreported byte is lost. The 3-layer permanent fix: (Layer 1) canonical checkpoint store `tools/subagent_checkpoint.py` appends JSONL to `.omx/state/subagent_progress.jsonl` under `fcntl.flock(LOCK_EX)` per Catalog #131 bare-write discipline; schema = `{subagent_id, parent_id_or_session, lane_id, step, status (in_progress|blocked|complete), files_touched, next_action, notes, written_at_utc, pid, host}` — `lane_id` added 2026-05-14 codex finding #3 fix to enable predecessor-resume by lane id. (Layer 2) new CLAUDE.md "Mandatory crash-resume protocol" subsection under "Subagent coherence-by-default" mandates that every long-running subagent (a) read predecessor checkpoint at start, (b) checkpoint every ~10 tool uses or major milestone, (c) checkpoint `--step complete` on completion. **Codex finding #3 fix (2026-05-14)** added three predecessor-resume query paths to `tools/subagent_checkpoint.py read`: `--parent-id-or-session <id>` (resolve by predecessor's parent session), `--latest-incomplete` (return the most-recent non-complete record), `--lane-id <lane>` (resolve by structured `lane_id` field OR notes-substring fallback). The original `--subagent-id` exact-match flow is preserved for backward compat. (Layer 3) this STRICT preflight gate scans the last 50 commits, intersects with `.omx/state/commit-serializer.log` (so only TRUE subagent commits per Catalog #117 are in scope), and refuses any commit body that does NOT mention the canonical checkpoint surface (`tools/subagent_checkpoint.py` / `subagent_checkpoint.py` / `subagent_progress.jsonl` / `checkpoint discipline honored`) OR carry a same-line `# CHECKPOINT_DISCIPLINE_WAIVED:<reason>` waiver for short subagents (≤5 tool uses estimate). Bare `CHECKPOINT_DISCIPLINE_WAIVED` keyword and placeholder `<reason>` literal are both rejected so the catalog's own example phrasing cannot self-waive. Sister of Catalog #117 (`check_subagent_commit_serializer_uses_lock` — the gate that establishes which commits are subagent-attributed) + Catalog #119 (`check_subagent_commits_have_co_author_trailer` — the gate that requires Claude attribution). Together the three close the subagent-attribution → checkpoint-discipline → co-author chain. **STRICT-FLIPPED 2026-05-14** per codex finding MEDIUM #2 fix with cutoff `_CHECK_206_DISCIPLINE_CUTOFF_UTC = "2026-05-14T11:00:00Z"`. Strict-flip is atomic per CLAUDE.md "Strict-flip atomicity rule": commits whose serializer-log `started_at_utc` is BEFORE the cutoff are exempt (legacy backfill window covering the originally-detected 13 in-flight subagent commits + sister-subagent OSS release prep + catalog-claim helper commits); commits AT OR AFTER the cutoff MUST comply or carry the waiver. Companion fix in `tools/claim_catalog_number.py --commit-via-serializer` now emits a `# CHECKPOINT_DISCIPLINE_WAIVED:` waiver in catalog-claim commit bodies so future claim commits do NOT trip the gate. Live count at strict-flip landing: 0 (post-cutoff). 90+ dedicated tests: 27 in `src/tac/tests/test_subagent_checkpoint.py` (schema validation, atomic append, latest/read API, CLI surface, 8-thread × 5-row concurrent-append safety) + 36 in `src/tac/tests/test_check_206_subagent_crash_resume_discipline.py` (canonical-token recognition, waiver-with-reason acceptance, bare-keyword + placeholder rejection, serializer-log intersection, dirty-commit flag, operator-commit excluded, strict-mode raises, live-repo regression guard, cutoff filter pre/post behavior, missing-`started_at_utc` legacy-treatment, orchestrator-callsite regression guard, post-cutoff strict-mode regression guard) + 27 in `src/tac/tests/test_subagent_checkpoint_resume_lookup.py` (the codex #3 fix: structured `lane_id` field, `read_checkpoints_by_parent`, `read_checkpoints_by_lane` with notes-substring fallback, `latest_incomplete_checkpoint`, CLI `read --parent-id-or-session` / `--latest-incomplete` / `--lane-id`, mutually-exclusive query-mode validation, end-to-end successor-with-different-id crash-resume scenario, CLI subprocess invocation). Memory: `feedback_subagent_crash_resume_discipline_landed_20260514.md` + `feedback_codex_3_findings_fix_landed_20260514.md`. Lane: `lane_subagent_crash_resume_discipline_20260514` + `lane_codex_3_findings_fix_20260514`.

207. `check_no_unguarded_rmtree_in_build_tools` — Codex 3-findings fix HIGH (2026-05-14). Refuses `tools/build_*.py` / `tools/promote_*.py` whose enclosing function BOTH (a) references a `--force`-style flag (`args.force` / `arguments.force` / `.force` / `options.force` / `namespace.force`) AND (b) calls `shutil.rmtree(...)` (or bare-imported `rmtree(...)`) WITHOUT a recognized guard token (`_assert_rmtree_safe`, `assert_rmtree_safe`, `rmtree_within_namespace`, `ensure_within_namespace`, `ensure_under_results_namespace`, `validate_force_delete_path`, `guard_force_delete`, `UnsafeRmtreeRefusedError`) in the same function body. Empirical anchor: `tools/build_pr101_nonlocal_sweep_packets.py:712-714` piped a user-controlled `--out-dir` straight into `shutil.rmtree` when `--force` was set; a typo such as `--out-dir . --force` would have recursively deleted the entire repo, and an absolute path outside the repo would have deleted unrelated user state. Companion fix landed in the same commit-batch: `_assert_rmtree_safe(out_dir)` validator + `_write_tool_owned_manifest(out_dir)` helper + `UnsafeRmtreeRefusedError` typed exception in `tools/build_pr101_nonlocal_sweep_packets.py`. The validator refuses (1) repo root, (2) `$HOME`, (3) absolute non-repo paths, (4) parent-directory escapes, (5) the `experiments/results/` namespace root itself, (6) any path NOT under `<repo_root>/experiments/results/`, AND (7) any namespaced path that does NOT contain the tool-owned manifest `build_pr101_nonlocal_sweep_manifest.json` (or legacy `manifest.json`). Same-line waiver `# RMTREE_UNGUARDED_OK:<reason>` honored on the rmtree call line; placeholder `<reason>` literal rejected. The narrow scope (only fires when the function references a `--force` flag) avoids false positives on the many legitimate rmtree calls that recursively clear a freshly-created tmp/staging directory whose path is computed entirely inside the function. Sister of Catalog #154 (`check_experiments_results_gc_helper_is_canonical` — extincts the same bug class for the canonical `experiments/results/` GC helper) and Catalog #156 (`check_gc_helper_refuses_delete_on_tracked_paths` — defense-in-depth against tracked-path deletion). Together they extinct the user-controlled-recursive-deletion bug class across the canonical GC surface, the public release prep surface, and the build-tool surface. Canonical helper file `tools/build_pr101_nonlocal_sweep_packets.py` is on the `_CHECK_207_CANONICAL_HELPERS` allowlist (its rmtree IS guarded by the helper it itself defines); future canonical helpers should be added there. 219. `check_archive_promotion_blocked_by_mdl_density_above_threshold` — Z1 empirical revision self-protection 2026-05-14 (operator decision #5 from `feedback_z1_mdl_ablation_landed_20260514.md`). Prefix matching against full shas supports the canonical lane-registry abbreviation `87ec7ca5...492b5`. Exemption cascades (HNeRV parity discipline lesson 7): (a) `lane_class` ∈ {substrate_class_shift, substrate_engineering, research_substrate}; (b) `target_modes` containing `research_substrate` / `research_only`; (c) `notes` body containing `research_only=true` / `lane_class=substrate_engineering` / `substrate_engineering_exception`; (d) same-line `# MDL_DENSITY_OVER_THRESHOLD_OK:<rationale>` waiver on the lane-registry notes / evidence body (placeholder `<reason>` literal rejected). Bug class: a lane promoted to L2+ with archive bytes whose MDL density is empirically class-saturated is by definition in the within-class trap per Z1 ablation — any further within-class encoder/codec refinement yields diminishing returns; the operator's next dispatch should route to a class-shift architecture (predictive-receiver / cooperative-receiver / foveation / Wyner-Ziv) not another within-class bolt-on. Initial wire-in is `strict=False` (warn-only) per CLAUDE.md "Strict-flip atomicity rule" — live count at landing: 0 (4 MDL-evidenced L2+ lanes all declare `lane_class=substrate_engineering` in their notes which exempts them as operator-acknowledged baselines; A1 and PR106 r2 ARE the empirical anchors that produced the threshold). Together they extinct the "class-saturated archive silently promoted to L2+" bug class. The cathedral autopilot ranker (`tools/cathedral_autopilot_autonomous_loop.py`) was updated in the same commit-batch with the sister `adjust_predicted_delta_for_mdl_density` + `adjust_predicted_delta_for_class_shift` + `apply_z1_empirical_revision_to_candidate_delta` helpers and a new `apply_z1_empirical_revision=True` default kwarg on `rank_candidates` — class-saturated candidates (density > 0.95) have their predicted ΔS floored at -0.005; trending (0.90-0.95) get a 50% penalty; class-shift literature anchors (cooperative-receiver / predictive-coding / Tishby-Zaslavsky / Atick-Redlich / Rao-Ballard / Wyner-Ziv / MDL-IBPS / Slepian-Wolf / time_traveler / foveation / Information Bottleneck) receive additive -0.01 to -0.03 ΔS rewards. The re-ranked autopilot queue v2 lives at `.omx/state/autopilot_candidate_queue_v2_post_z1_revision_20260514.jsonl` and promotes time_traveler / DARTS-SuperNet / C6 MDL-IBPS class-shift candidates to top-3 ahead of all within-A1-class sidecar bolt-ons. 44 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_check_219_mdl_density_promotion_gate.py` covering helper unit-tests (sha context detection / MDL result discovery / sha token extraction with abbreviated forms / sha prefix matching / exemption cascades / waiver mechanism) and end-to-end gate behavior (passes when no registry / passes when no MDL evidence / flags lane with density 0.99 / skips L1 lanes / skips exempt lanes via lane_class / skips waived lanes / threshold boundary 0.90 strict > / multiple lanes flagged together / abbreviated sha form matched / strict raises with Catalog #219 message / strict silent on clean repo / live-repo regression guard). Plus 32 dedicated ranker tests in `src/tac/tests/test_cathedral_autopilot_z1_revision.py` covering the within-class trap penalty, the class-shift reward stacking, the JSONL backward-compat loader, and A1-baseline / PR106-baseline / C6-MDL-IBPS / time-traveler-L5 regression cases. Memory: `feedback_mdl_density_gate_and_autopilot_ranker_landed_20260514.md`. Lane: `lane_mdl_density_gate_and_autopilot_ranker_20260514`.

221. `check_auth_eval_result_artifacts_fail_closed_for_score_claims` — D1/Z1/FES/PacketIR evidence-surface hardening 2026-05-14. Refuses generated `experiments/results/**/auth_eval_roundtrip_results.json` artifacts whose top-level or row-level `score_claim` is anything other than `false`, whose rows are rank/kill eligible, or whose rows lack the fail-closed `result_review_blockers` (`roundtrip_matrix_is_command_planner_not_claim_surface`, `requires_separate_auth_eval_result_review_before_score_claim`). Also refuses PacketIR `closure.json` artifacts with a `contest_cpu` axis row that lacks explicit CPU-diagnostic blockers (`not_contest_cuda_axis`, `cpu_axis_not_rank_or_kill_authority`, `requires_cuda_cpu_policy_review`) in both `promotion_blockers` and `rank_or_kill_blockers`. Bug class: command-planner/recovery artifacts can preserve useful `[contest-CUDA]` and `[contest-CPU]` signal while still being non-authoritative; if nested rows leak `score_claim=true` or CPU rows carry empty blockers, downstream autopilot, rank/kill, or operator review can promote a planning artifact or CPU diagnostic as if it were reviewed CUDA frontier evidence. Companion fixes landed in `src/tac/auth_eval_roundtrip_matrix.py` (row-level `score_claim=false`, `contest_axis_anchor`, `score_claim_possible_after_result_review`, review blockers), `src/tac/analysis/scorer_conditional_mdl.py` (missing eval archive identity is weak custody, not `custody_match=true`), and `src/tac/packetir_exact_closure.py` (CPU-axis diagnostic blockers). STRICT-from-byte-one per CLAUDE.md "Bugs must be permanently fixed AND self-protected against"; live count at landing: 0 after regenerating the FES roundtrip results and PR106/R2 PacketIR closure. 7 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_preflight_auth_eval_result_artifact_guards.py` cover row-level score-claim leakage, missing review blockers, CPU-axis blocker omissions, strict-mode raise, clean-artifact pass, live-repo regression, and orchestrator-callsite strict=True wiring. Ledger: `.omx/research/d1_z1_score_lowering_hardening_20260514_codex.md`. Lane: `lane_d1_z1_fes_packetir_hardening_20260514`.

222. `check_scorer_loader_assignment_order` — C6/score-aware trainer self-protection 2026-05-14. Refuses any source file under `src/tac/`, `experiments/`, or `tools/` that assigns canonical scorer loader outputs in reversed order, e.g. `seg_scorer, pose_scorer = load_differentiable_scorers(...)`, `segnet, posenet = load_default_scorers(...)`, or the same pattern around `load_scorers(...)`. The canonical API returns `(posenet, segnet)`; reversing it feeds SegNet into the PoseNet slot and usually fails only inside `scorer_loss_terms_btchw` when a tensor is indexed as `fp_out["pose"]`. Anchor: C6 MDL-IBPS Modal smoke `substrate_c6_e4_mdl_ibps_modal_t4_dispatch_20260514T142426Z__smoke__100ep` failed after scorer load with `TypeError: new(): invalid data type 'str'` at `src/tac/losses/core.py:740`, caused by `experiments/train_substrate_c6_e4_mdl_ibps.py` assigning `seg_scorer, pose_scorer = load_differentiable_scorers(...)`. Companion fix changed the trainer and its real-scorer test to `pose_scorer, seg_scorer = ...`. Same-line waiver `# SCORER_LOADER_ORDER_OK:<reason>` is accepted only for deliberate nonstandard wrappers; placeholder `<reason>` is rejected. STRICT-from-byte-one per CLAUDE.md "Bugs must be permanently fixed AND self-protected against"; live count at landing: 0 after patching C6. 7 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_preflight_scorer_loader_assignment_order.py` cover reversed differentiable/default loader assignments, clean order, waiver semantics, strict raise, live-repo regression, and `preflight_all` strict wiring. Ledger: `.omx/research/d1_z1_score_lowering_hardening_20260514_codex.md`. Lane: `lane_c6_scorer_loader_order_hardening_20260514`.

223. `check_substrate_auth_eval_invocations_use_current_cli` — C6/auth-eval CLI self-protection 2026-05-14. Refuses any `experiments/train_substrate_*.py` trainer that invokes `experiments/contest_auth_eval.py` with stale flags such as `--archive-zip` / `--output-json`, or with a direct invocation missing any of the current required score-custody flags `--archive`, `--inflate-sh`, and `--json-out`, unless it routes through the canonical `tac.substrates._shared.smoke_auth_eval_gate.gate_auth_eval_call(...)`. Bug class: C6 reached post-train archive/runtime emission but its inline auth-eval command used stale argparse flags and no emitted runtime `inflate.sh`; the trainer caught the failure, left `auth_eval_score=None`, wrote stats, printed DONE, and returned rc=0, letting the remote driver potentially emit a false `[contest-CUDA]` completion marker from trainer rc alone. Companion fix routes C6 through `gate_auth_eval_call(...)`, writes fail-closed stats authority fields (`auth_eval_score_axis`, `auth_eval_score_claim_valid`, `promotion_eligible=false`, `rank_or_kill_eligible=false`, `result_review_blockers`), makes the remote C6 driver validate `auth_eval_score_claim_valid=true` + `contest_cuda` before writing `LANE_C6_MDL_IBPS_DONE [contest-CUDA]`, and hardens `detect_hardware_substrate(axis="cpu")` so local macOS CPU artifacts are labeled `macos_arm64` rather than `linux_x86_64_modal_cpu`. Same-line waiver `# AUTH_EVAL_CLI_OK:<reason>` is reserved for reviewed non-runtime fixtures only; production substrate trainers should use the canonical gate. STRICT-from-byte-one; live count at landing: 0 after patching C6. Dedicated tests: `src/tac/tests/test_preflight_substrate_auth_eval_cli_contract.py` plus CPU-substrate regression tests in `src/tac/tests/test_trainer_skeleton.py`. Ledger: `.omx/research/d1_z1_score_lowering_hardening_20260514_codex.md`. Lane: `lane_c6_auth_eval_cli_fail_closed_hardening_20260514`.

224. `check_modal_training_image_includes_hard_runtime_deps` extension — Modal training DALI/NVML runtime-env self-protection 2026-05-14. Tightens Catalog #203 so the canonical Modal training dispatcher must carry not only hard archive entropy dependencies, but also `DALI_DISABLE_NVML` and `PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True` through the Modal image, generated `env.sh`, and subprocess environment. Empirical anchors: D1 R4 T4 smoke `fc-01KRKBF28G2M3N73FS7PDCB6AZ` and C6 patched smoke `fc-01KRKG566Z2F48CVCGF8JFA0S1` both inflated successfully and then failed inside upstream `evaluate.py` / DALI with `nvml error (999): A nvml internal driver error occurred`. Modal auth-eval and lane-specific wrappers already set `DALI_DISABLE_NVML=1`; the generic `experiments/modal_train_lane.py` image/env path lagged and could burn full inflate time before a provider NVML crash. Companion fix imports shared constants from `tac.deploy.modal.runtime`, threads them into `image.env(...)`, `env.sh`, and the lane subprocess `env`, and keeps scores fail-closed if the provider still crashes. Dedicated tests extend `src/tac/tests/test_check_203_modal_training_image_runtime_deps.py` and `src/tac/tests/test_modal_train_lane_hardening.py`. Ledger: `.omx/research/d1_z1_score_lowering_hardening_20260514_codex.md`. Lane: `lane_modal_training_dali_nvml_env_hardening_20260514`.

225. `tools/claim_lane_dispatch.py` terminal-status vocabulary hardening — dispatch ledger churn prevention 2026-05-14. Adds `timed_out` to the shared terminal-prefix vocabulary in both the CLI helper and `tac.deploy.claims`. Empirical anchor: D4 rows with statuses `timed_out_3600s_a10g_smoke` and `timed_out_3600s_t4_smoke` were operationally terminal, but `tools/claim_lane_dispatch.py summary` counted them as active because the terminal vocabulary only recognized `failed_...` timeout closures. That inflated active claims from 5 to 7 and risked false same-lane blocking or duplicate manual cleanup. Companion fix keeps existing `failed_modal_training_timeout` rows valid and additionally treats `timed_out_*` as terminal. Dedicated tests extend `src/tac/tests/test_claim_lane_dispatch.py` and `src/tac/tests/test_deploy_claims_active_row.py`. Ledger: `.omx/research/d1_z1_score_lowering_hardening_20260514_codex.md`. Lane: `lane_dispatch_claim_terminal_timeout_vocabulary_hardening_20260514`.

227. `check_substrate_class_promotion_requires_tier_c_evidence` — Tier C empirical revision self-protection 2026-05-14 (per `feedback_mdl_ablation_tier_c_ibps1_landed_20260514.md` + `project_c6_substrate_class_shift_first_empirical_confirmation_tier_c_20260514.md` operator decision #4 RECOMMENDED-LAND-NEXT). Sister of Catalog #219 (Tier A density gate). Where #219 refuses L2+ promotion when Tier A density > 0.90, #227 refuses substrate-class-shift PROMOTION (lanes claiming `lane_class` ∈ class-shift tokens OR `notes` referencing class-shift literature anchors) at L2+ WITHOUT Tier C empirical evidence. Bug class: Tier A is brotli-saturated at the byte layer (any fp16-weight + brotli archive sits at Tier A density ~0.99) and structurally CANNOT discriminate substrate class. A lane can claim ACROSS-CLASS lineage via its lane_class / notes / literature_anchor field WITHOUT empirically demonstrating the class-shift signature via Tier C post-decode perturbation. Per the C6 5ep empirical anchor, the dispositive test IS Tier C — the latent Δscore at σ=1.0 must be sub-0.05 AND state_dict perturbation must show a knee+plateau structural signature. Acceptance cascades: (a) lane evidence/notes contain a Tier C token (`tier_c` / `Tier C` / `mdl_tier_c` / etc.) — the operator has explicitly acknowledged Tier C either ran OR is in flight; (b) lane's archive sha256 is in the Tier C ablation index (an MDL ablation JSON with non-empty `tier_c` rows exists for the archive); (c) lane carries a same-line `# TIER_C_EVIDENCE_PENDING_OK:<rationale>` waiver — reserved for pre-empirical lanes (still in design / 5ep / pre-Tier-C-feasibility); placeholder `<reason>` literal rejected; (d) lane is exempt via `lane_class` ∈ {`substrate_engineering`, `research_substrate`} OR `target_modes` ∈ {`research_substrate`, `research_only`} OR notes containing `research_only=true` / `substrate_engineering_exception`. NARROWER than Catalog #219's exemption — #227 does NOT exempt `substrate_class_shift` lanes (those ARE the gated lanes). Class-shift tokens scanned: `substrate_class_shift` / `predictive_receiver` / `cooperative_receiver` / `foveation` (lane_class) + `MDL-IBPS` / `Wyner-Ziv` / `Tishby-Zaslavsky` / `Atick-Redlich` / `Rao-Ballard` / `cooperative-receiver` / `predictive-coding` / `Hafner` / `DreamerV3` / `Ha-Schmidhuber` / `world_model` / `time_traveler` / etc. (notes literature). Companion landings in same commit-batch: (1) `tools/mdl_scorer_conditional_ablation.py::aggregate_mdl_estimate` now computes `mdl_tier_c_density_estimate` + `mdl_tier_c_substrate_class_verdict` + `mdl_tier_c_curve_knee_signal` + `mdl_tier_c_latent_sigma1_delta` from any non-empty `tier_c` list (composite density = mean of two logistic-squashed signals: latent_sigma1 = |Δscore_latents@σ=1.0| / (latent_sigma1 + 0.05); curve_knee = |Δscore_sd@σ=0.1| / |Δscore_sd@σ=0.01| / (curve_knee + 3.0); thresholds density ≥ 0.70 → within-class, density ≤ 0.30 → across-class, else indeterminate); (2) `tools/cathedral_autopilot_autonomous_loop.py` extends CandidateRow with `mdl_tier_c_density` + `composition_alpha` fields + adds `adjust_predicted_delta_for_mdl_tier_c_density` (within-class density ≥ 0.70 floors at -0.005 like Tier A; trending 0.50-0.70 halves; across-class ≤ 0.30 applies -0.01 bonus matching the Tier-C-empirical-anchor across-class reward) + `adjust_predicted_delta_for_composition_alpha` (α > 0.7 ADDITIVE / 0.3-0.7 SUB-ADDITIVE halve / ≤ 0.3 SATURATING floor at -0.005) + `load_substrate_composition_alpha_index` reader of canonical `.omx/state/substrate_composition_matrix.json` posterior surface + `apply_substrate_composition_matrix_to_candidates` populator + extends `_CLASS_SHIFT_LITERATURE_TOKENS` with `Ha-Schmidhuber` / `Hafner` / `DreamerV3` / `Dreamer V3` / `Dreamer-V3` per C1 council RETAIN decision (`project_c1_world_model_revision_SUPERSEDED_by_council_unfair_probe_finding_20260514.md`); (3) `apply_z1_empirical_revision_to_candidate_delta` composition order Tier A → Tier C → class-shift → composition_alpha. Initial wire-in is warn-only per CLAUDE.md "Strict-flip atomicity rule"; live count at landing: 0 (673 lanes scanned, 0 class-shift L2+ lanes without Tier C evidence). Strict-flip planned once 0 violations are confirmed AND converged 100ep+ Tier C anchor lands on at least one class-shift substrate (C6 IBPS1 100ep at Modal A100 per HARVEST-AND-Z1 op-routable #1). Sister of Catalog #219 (Tier A density gate; brotli-saturated layer) + Catalog #125 (subagent landing 6-hook wire-in non-negotiable — this gate IS hook #4 for the Tier C result loop). Together they extinct the "class-shift claim without empirical Tier C evidence" bug class. Per CLAUDE.md "Forbidden premature KILL without research exhaustion": the gate REFUSES promotion of an unproven class-shift CLAIM but does NOT kill the lane; the operator can either run Tier C OR add the waiver for pre-empirical scope. 48 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_check_227_substrate_class_promotion_requires_tier_c.py` covering helper unit-tests (class-shift detection via lane_class / notes / literature; tier_c evidence acceptance via token / sha-index; waiver semantics with placeholder rejection; sha-context discovery; exempt-cascade narrower than #219; tier_c discovery skips empty rows) + end-to-end gate behavior (passes when no registry / no class-shift lanes / L1 lanes / gate not satisfied; flags L2 class-shift without Tier C; passes with token/sha/waiver; skips research_only lanes; multi-lane aggregation; strict-mode raises with Catalog #227 + Tier C disambiguator message; verbose summary) + live-repo regression guard + orchestrator-callsite wire-in regression guard. Plus 42 ranker tests in `src/tac/tests/test_cathedral_autopilot_tier_c_and_composition.py` covering Tier C density penalty/reward bands (within-class floor / trending halve / across-class bonus / indeterminate / None / non-numeric / threshold boundaries) + composition_alpha bands (additive / sub-additive / saturating / None / non-numeric / T1-F Z3×C6 realistic value) + composition matrix loader (missing / canonical / most-recent / refuses score_claim / malformed JSON / live T1-F artifact) + composition populator (populates / sorted key lookup / skips single-substrate / handles empty) + C1 council literature token retention (Hafner / DreamerV3 / Ha-Schmidhuber) + full ranker stack composition (Tier A + Tier C + class-shift + composition correct ordering) + JSONL loader backward-compat (new fields read / invalid fields fall back / legacy rows unchanged) + rank_candidates integration (within-class loses to across-class / disable-z1-revision preserves legacy order) + live regression guards (v2 queue still loads / constants pinned). Plus 11 Tier C aggregator tests in `src/tac/tests/test_mdl_scorer_conditional_ablation.py` covering empty/canonical/within-class signatures + JSON round-trip + real C6 5ep archive regression. Memory: `feedback_autopilot_tier_c_integration_catalog_227_landed_20260514.md`. Lane: `lane_autopilot_tier_c_integration_catalog_227_20260514`.

233. `check_l1_to_l2_promotion_canonical_4_gate` — L1→L2 substrate-class promotion canonical 4-gate enforcement self-protection 2026-05-14 (per Grand Council omnibus commit `7872c9f4b` Decision 8 binding verdict, PROCEED Option D, 11/11 unanimous; lines 336-388 of `.omx/research/grand_council_omnibus_design_decisions_20260514.md`). Operationalizes the council's L1→L2 substrate-class promotion canonical: (1) **Smoke green** (rc=0 + auth-eval JSON parseable); (2) **Tier C MDL density measured** (`tools/mdl_scorer_conditional_ablation.py` on the smoke artifact); (3) **100ep auth-eval anchor** with byte-deterministic archive (sha256 stable across re-runs); (4) **Custody validated per Catalog #127** (`evidence_grade` matches axis + hardware). The conditional 5th gate (probe-disambiguator built if 2+ defensible interpretations exist) is enforced separately via Catalog #125 hook #6. Bug class anchor (per omnibus ledger §"Math + rationale" lines 375-381): each gate is structurally non-redundant; together they prevent THREE failure modes: "substrate trained but never validated" (smoke gate), "substrate validated but within-class saturated" (Tier C gate), "score claim without custody" (100ep auth-eval + Catalog #127 gates). The gate scans `.omx/state/lane_registry.json` for in-scope substrate L2+ lanes (via `_CHECK_233_IN_SCOPE_ID_SUBSTRINGS` mirroring Catalog #220's set: `substrate_` / `_substrate_` / `_polytope_` / `_sidecar_` / `_overlay_` / `yucr_` / `d1_segnet` / `d2_` / `d4_` / `lane_a1_` / `_hnerv_` / `_nerv_` / `_lora_` / `wavelet_residual` / `siren_residual` / `coord_mlp_residual`); for each in-scope L2+ lane it concatenates `notes` + every gate's `evidence` string and refuses the lane unless ALL FOUR gate-token sets are present (`_CHECK_233_SMOKE_GREEN_TOKENS` / `_CHECK_233_TIER_C_TOKENS` / `_CHECK_233_AUTH_EVAL_100EP_TOKENS` / `_CHECK_233_CUSTODY_VALIDATOR_TOKENS`). Acceptance cascades: (a) lane is exempt via `lane_class=substrate_engineering` / `lane_class=research_substrate` / `target_modes` containing `research_substrate` / `research_only` / notes containing `research_only=true` / `substrate_engineering_exception` (these lanes are explicitly NOT contest-promotion eligible per HNeRV parity discipline lessons 2 + 7); (b) lane carries a same-line `# L1_L2_PROMOTION_CANONICAL_OK:<rationale>` waiver in registry notes / evidence (placeholder `<reason>` literal rejected); (c) all four gate tokens appear in concatenated lane text. Sister of Catalog #127 (`check_authoritative_tag_requires_custody_metadata` — the per-call-site custody validator routing gate; #233 is its lane-registry-level enforcement complement) + Catalog #220 (`check_substrate_l1_scaffold_no_byte_addition_without_operational_score_improvement_mechanism` — refuses L1+ scaffolds adding bytes without operational mechanism; #233 refuses L2+ promotion claims lacking the canonical 4-gate evidence) + Catalog #227 (`check_substrate_class_promotion_requires_tier_c_evidence` — refuses L2+ class-shift lanes lacking Tier C; #233 extends to ALL L2+ substrate lanes and enforces 4 gates instead of single Tier C). Together the four close the substrate L2+ promotion bug-class meta-surface: per-call-site custody routing (#127) + L1 scaffold byte-addition (#220) + L2+ class-shift Tier C (#227) + L2+ all-substrate 4-gate canonical (#233). Initial wire-in is warn-only per CLAUDE.md "Strict-flip atomicity rule" + the council's operator-routable checkpoint #2 ("any L2-claimed lane WITHOUT all 4 gates is forensically reviewed and downgraded if necessary"). Live count at landing: 6 violations across 17 in-scope substrate L2+ lanes (11 exempt via substrate_engineering / research_only opt-out, 6 unexempt: `lane_12_nerv_mask_codec`, `lane_a1_inflate_time_bias_correction_sweep`, `lane_a1_segnet_boundary_smoothing_inflate`, `lane_pr106_latent_sidecar_r2_pr101_grammar_contest_cpu`, `lane_b1_nerv_enc_dec_x_magic_codec_a1_20260512`, `lane_macos_cpu_substrate_canvas_sweep_20260513`). Strict-flip pending the operator-routed audit-and-downgrade sweep per the council's checkpoint #2 — each flagged lane is either backfilled with the missing canonical-gate evidence into its registry notes, downgraded to L1 if the 4-gate canonical cannot yet be satisfied, re-classified as `research_only=true` / `lane_class=substrate_engineering` if explicitly NOT contest-promotion eligible, OR carries a council-reviewed `# L1_L2_PROMOTION_CANONICAL_OK:<rationale>` waiver. Per CLAUDE.md "Forbidden premature KILL without research exhaustion": the gate REFUSES L2+ promotion of unproven canonical claims but does NOT kill the lane. 60 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_check_233_l1_to_l2_promotion_canonical.py` covering helper unit-tests (in-scope classifier with canonical id-substring set + empty-id rejection + non-substrate id rejection; exempt classifier across all 4 exemption surfaces — lane_class / target_modes / research_only-in-notes / substrate_engineering_exception; concatenate-lane-text helper notes-plus-gates aggregation; waiver semantics with rationale accepted + `<reason>` + `<rationale>` placeholder rejection + inline-form acceptance + no-marker case; case-insensitive token-substring contains-helper preserving `_` vs space; 4-gate evaluator returning per-gate booleans across 5 single-token-only scenarios + 1 three-of-four scenario) + end-to-end gate behavior (passes when no lane registry / no L2+ substrate lanes / lane with all 4 gates in notes / lane with all 4 gates split across gate.evidence strings; flags lane missing each individual gate with the EXACT `(N) <gate-name>` message; flags lane missing all 4 gates with "missing 4 of 4" aggregation; L1 lane skipped per L1→L2 scope; L3 lane also audited per L2+ scope; research_only / substrate_engineering / research_substrate target-mode lanes exempt; waiver with rationale passes lane / placeholder rejected; multi-lane aggregation; corrupt registry handled gracefully; registry without lanes list handled; non-dict lane entries skipped; string repo_root accepted; verbose output lists per-lane state) + strict-mode (raises with PreflightError mentioning Catalog #233 + council Decision 8 + 4-gate canonical / silent on clean) + token-constants pinned (smoke / tier_c / auth_eval / custody / exempt-lane-class / exempt-target-mode token sets all importable + canonical phrasings present) + orchestrator-callsite strict=False wire-in regression guard + live-repo regression guard (upper bound 20 violations until operator-routed audit sweep brings count to 0) + Catalog #185 sister regression (gate function callable via globals) + signature regression (kwargs-only) + anti-regression (every canonical in-scope substring classifies as in-scope) + violation-message specificity (lane missing exactly 2 of 4 gates names exactly the missing 2, NOT the satisfied 2) + docstring anchors (4 gates verbatim from council verdict + omnibus commit `7872c9f4b` + "Option D" cited). 228. `check_substrate_trainer_consumes_f3_cache_when_flag_declared` — F3 GTScorerCache trainer-side consumption self-protection 2026-05-14 (F3-BACKPORT-WAVE-V2 op-routable #5 from `feedback_f3_backport_wave_v2_eleven_trainers_landed_20260514.md`). Refuses substrate trainers under `experiments/train_substrate_*.py` that declare the RESERVED `--enable-gt-scorer-cache` Tier-1 optimization flag (Catalog #151 manifest entry) but do NOT consume the GTScorerCache primitive (`gt_cache.lookup(` + `gt_pose_batch=` threading) in their hot loop. Bug class: a future trainer refactor could silently remove the wire-in (replace canonical `_build_optimized_training_context` with manual `loss_fn(...)`); regression would not surface until a Modal/Lightning smoke timeout on contest-resolution training. The gate delegates verdict classification to the canonical helper at `tools/check_f3_trainer_actionable.py` (`_classify` function) so the gate + operator-facing reproducer stay in lock-step. Only `NEEDS_F3_BACKPORT` and `NEEDS_F3_BACKPORT_PLUS_TIER1_FLAGS` are refused; every other canonical verdict (`ALREADY_WIRED` / `D1_SEGNET_SIDECAR_NO_HOT_LOOP` / `FULL_MAIN_STUB_NO_SCORER_HOT_LOOP` / `Z3_V1_RATE_ONLY_SCORERS_IDENTITY` / `SMOKE_SYNTHETIC_SCORERS_FULL_STUB` / `NEEDS_SUBSTRATE_F3_WIRE_IN` / `RESERVED_FLAG_BUT_NO_SCORER_HOT_LOOP` / `RESERVED_FLAG_NOT_CONSUMED` / `UNKNOWN_STATE_INVESTIGATE` / `NO_TRAINER_FILE`) is a structurally legitimate non-actionable state. 218. `check_substrate_reconstruct_methods_support_mini_batch` — D4 OOM fix self-protection 2026-05-14 (anchor: fc-01KRK9RKD3QV4C276Y5KXFMF65 — D4 Modal T4 smoke at 2026-05-14T13:10:25Z returned rc=1 elapsed 121s with CUDA OOM at `synthesize_frame_0` F.interpolate residual upsample with 600-pair batch). Refuses substrate reconstruct methods under `src/tac/substrates/*/architecture.py` and `src/tac/substrates/*/frame*_synthesis.py` and `src/tac/substrates/*/synthesis.py` whose name matches a canonical per-pair-batch reconstruct pattern (`reconstruct_pair`, `reconstruct_pairs`, `reconstruct_batch`, `archive_build_pass`) WITHOUT one of the following: (a) a mini-batch kwarg in the signature (`pair_indices`, `chunk_size`, `indices`, `batch_indices`, `minibatch_indices`); (b) a `torch.no_grad` / `torch.inference_mode` body context OR `@torch.no_grad` / `@torch.inference_mode` decorator; (c) a same-line `# RECONSTRUCT_BATCH_OOM_OK:<reason>` waiver (placeholder `<reason>` literal rejected so the docstring cannot self-waive). 213. `check_comma2k19_downloads_route_through_canonical_cache` — DP1 Comma2k19 auto-load self-protection 2026-05-14 (operator directive *"you can download the dataset locally and configure the chunks dir to be fed dynamically cached with a log incremental generator"*). Refuses any caller under `src/tac/`, `tools/`, `experiments/`, `scripts/` that fetches Comma2k19 chunk bytes (URL fragments: `raw.githubusercontent.com/commaai/comma2k19`, `github.com/commaai/comma2k19/raw`, `github.com/commaai/comma2k19/blob`, `github.com/commaai/comma2k19/archive`, `academictorrents.com/details/65a2fbc964078aff62076ff4e103f18b951c5ddb`, `academictorrents.com/download/65a2fbc964078aff62076ff4e103f18b951c5ddb`, `comma2k19.tar`, `comma2k19.zip`) via download-call tokens (`urlopen`, `urlretrieve`, `requests.get`/`post`/`head`, `httpx.get`/`post`, `Session().get`, shell `wget `/`curl `/`aria2c `, `transmission-cli`, `transmission-remote`) OUTSIDE the canonical `tac.substrates.pretrained_driving_prior.local_chunk_cache.Comma2k19LocalCache` helper. Bug class: ad-hoc downloads silently bypass (1) URL pinning (the canonical `DEFAULT_CHUNK_MANIFEST` is the single source-of-truth — a tampered mirror or stale URL can leak into a non-canonical fetch), (2) SHA-256 integrity verification (the cache hashes every downloaded chunk against the pinned expected sha; bare downloads cannot detect tampering / bit-rot / MitM), (3) license-tag propagation (`license="MIT"` + `source_url` stamped into cache meta and forwarded to codebook provenance per Catalog #210), (4) disk-budget LRU eviction (`max_disk_gb` cap + oldest-LRU eviction; bare fetches can fill the Modal/Vast.ai worker disk and crash the dispatch), (5) `/tmp` avoidance (partial downloads land in `<cache_dir>/.downloads/` and are `os.replace`-d atomically per CLAUDE.md "Forbidden /tmp paths"). Scope: scans `.py` + `.sh` files in the 4 dirs; excludes tests / vendored intake clones / OSS export mirrors / `experiments/results/` / `reports/raw/` / `build/lib/`. Per-line detection: a line with a Comma2k19 URL fragment AND a download-call token within ±2 lines (handles multi-line argument lists). Acceptance: (a) ±15-line window contains one of the canonical-cache tokens (`Comma2k19LocalCache`, `local_chunk_cache`, `from tac.substrates.pretrained_driving_prior.local_chunk_cache`, `from tac.substrates.pretrained_driving_prior import`, `.fetch_chunk(`, `.fetch_chunks(`); (b) same-line `# COMMA2K19_DOWNLOAD_OK:<rationale>` waiver with placeholder `<reason>` rejected (so the gate's docstring example cannot self-waive); (c) file-level `# COMMA2K19_DOWNLOAD_OK_FILE:<rationale>` waiver in the first 10 lines for legitimate one-off operator-reviewed download scripts (rare). Refuses any state of `src/tac/cost_band_calibration.py::classify_dispatch` that uses raw `>=` (CLOSED boundary) against UPGRADE-class ceilings (`PER_CLASS_SOFT_WALLCLOCK_CEILING_HR[upgrade_class]` or `PER_CLASS_SOFT_COST_CEILING_USD[upgrade_class]` where `upgrade_class` ∈ {`full`, `long_burn`, `eval`}). Bug class anchor: the R2 ledger BOYD-2 finding documented that the `classify_dispatch` numeric inference branches used CLOSED `>=` boundaries for the `long_burn` upgrade — at exactly 12.0h wallclock or 100 USD cost, the routing returned `long_burn` (Lightning A100 subscription, $50+ class) instead of `full` (Vast.ai 4090, $2-15 class). This is a 5-10x cost-class jump for a 1-second wallclock difference at the boundary. Per Boyd's convex-feasibility lens: "convex feasibility regions should have OPEN boundaries (strict `<` / `>`) when crossing them changes the cost class non-trivially. The existing CLOSED boundary creates a discontinuity in cost expectation at the boundary." Companion runtime fix changed lines 1208 (`>=` → `>`) for wallclock and 1220 (`>=` → `>`) for cost on the long_burn upgrade boundary. Smoke downgrade boundaries kept CLOSED `<=` because they route DOWNWARD to the cheaper class — at the smoke ceiling exactly the safer routing is `smoke` (no cost penalty for over-routing). **Codex round 8 MEDIUM in-place harden of #131 (2026-05-09, STRICT @ 0):**

131-v2. `check_no_bare_writes_to_shared_state` (in-place harden, no new catalog #) — Two in-place enhancements per codex round 8 MEDIUM (2026-05-09): (a) **AST-based path binding** (`_bare_write_collect_shared_vars_ast`) adds lowercase variables (e.g. `state_path = Path('.omx/state/foo.json')`), attribute paths (e.g. `self.foo_path = ...`), Path-joined RHS (`base / '.omx/state/foo.json'`), `AnnAssign` annotated bindings, and f-string bindings — every shape that the original all-caps regex collector silently missed; (b) **transactional-pattern requirement** — the previous lookback waiver accepted any lock token in the 20-line preceding window; the harden now requires EITHER a canonical helper invocation in the lookback (e.g. `update_active_jobs_locked(`, `register_instance(`, `posterior_update_locked(`) OR an explicit transactional pattern (write to `<path>.tmp` + `os.replace`) visible in the wider 30-line window. Lock-token alone is insufficient because partial writes are visible to readers even under the same lock and a process crash mid-write leaves a corrupt file. Canonical-helper detection skips `import ` / `from ` lines so an import statement bringing a helper name into the module does NOT falsely waive an unrelated bare write below. Same-line `# BARE_WRITE_OK:<reason>` waiver respected. Sister test files updated to reflect the new (correct) behavior: `test_131_locked_path_in_file_accepts` and `test_131_window_lock_token_accepts` (both in `src/tac/tests/test_preflight_proactive_custody_concurrency_audit.py`) now use the canonical transactional pattern; `test_check131_local_lock_context_accepted` (in `src/tac/tests/test_preflight_custody_validator_and_locked_writes.py`) updated similarly. New negative tests pin the hardened behavior: `test_131_lock_alone_no_atomic_replace_now_violation` + `test_check131_lock_alone_no_atomic_replace_is_violation_round8`. STRICT @ 0 (already strict in `preflight_all()`; no wire-in change). Live count after harden: 0. 15 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_codex_round78_check_131_harden_lowercase_and_atomic_replace.py`. 242. `check_register_substrate_contract_fields_canonical` — META-LAYER-SUBSTRATE-CONTRACT-AUTO-WIRE-SUBAGENT 2026-05-15. Companion to Catalog #241. Every file that uses `@register_substrate(...)` MUST register a contract that validates against the canonical 36-field `SubstrateContract` schema in `src/tac/substrate_registry/contract.py`. The check imports `tac.substrate_registry.example_template` (forces decorator execution) and re-runs `validate_all_registered()` over the in-memory `_REGISTERED_SUBSTRATES` dict. Defense-in-depth: the import-time validation is the primary surface (substrate fails to import on contract drift); this gate catches a registered-but-corrupted contract that may have escaped initial validation through an out-of-band mutation (e.g. `_REGISTERED_SUBSTRATES[id] = corrupted_object` test-only back door). The validator wraps both `SubstrateContractError` (field-level invariants) AND `TypeError` (missing-field / wrong-type construction errors) so any registry-mutation surface produces a string-formatted error. Validators enforce: id pattern `/^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/`; lane_id pattern `/^lane_[a-z0-9_]+_\d{8}$/`; target_modes non-empty + legal enum members; deployment_target legal; export_format legal; score_aware_loss legal; loc budgets > 0; runtime_dep_closure non-empty; OPERATIONAL ↔ runtime_overlay_consumed=True invariant (Catalog #220 mirror); SCAFFOLD with >1 KB byte addition refused (the research-substrate trap); recipe_smoke_only=True with cost_band_epochs > 100 refused (consistency invariant); recipe_canary_status=post_canary_dependent ↔ recipe_canary_dependency required; min_vram_gb >= 1; cost_band_epochs >= 1; hook_*=not_applicable_with_rationale REQUIRES non-empty rationale entry in hook_not_applicable_rationale; hook_probe_disambiguator=None requires rationale entry; hook_rationale dict keys restricted to legal hook field names. **STRICT-from-byte-one** per CLAUDE.md "Bugs must be permanently fixed AND self-protected against" + "Strict-flip atomicity rule" — live count at landing: 0 (only `tac.substrate_registry.example_template` uses the decorator and its contract is canonically clean). 5 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_check_241_242_meta_layer_gates.py` covering clean-registry-empty / strict-silent-on-clean / live-repo-zero-violations-regression-guard / strict-raises-when-corrupted / orchestrator-wires-strict-true. Sister of Catalog #241 (decorator-or-legacy-tagged adoption gate; #242 is the per-contract field-validation gate) + Catalog #176 (`check_strict_preflight_callsites_have_claude_md_catalog_row` — the META-meta gate that this entry satisfies for #242's strict-callsite). Memory: `feedback_meta_layer_substrate_contract_landed_20260515.md`. Cross-ref `.omx/research/substrate_meta_layer_design_20260515.md` § 5.3 (the contract validation invariants enumeration).

249. `check_no_misleading_device_named_output_directories` — phantom-score directory class permanent fix 2026-05-15 (per `feedback_misleading_directory_name_phantom_score_class_permanent_fix_landed_20260515.md`). Refuses any caller under `src/tac/`, `tools/`, `experiments/`, `scripts/` that constructs an auth-eval output JSON path or work directory whose filename device-token (`_cuda` / `_cpu` / `_mps`) is HARDCODED instead of derived from the actual auth-eval device. Empirical anchor: Z3 v2 FULL Modal A100 dispatch 2026-05-15T11:41:15Z. The trainer hardcoded `result_json_path = out_dir / "contest_auth_eval_cuda.json"` regardless of `auth_eval_device`. The Modal dispatcher injected `AUTH_EVAL_DEVICE=cpu` + `MODAL_AUTH_EVAL_ADVISORY_ONLY=1`. Result: `contest_auth_eval_cuda.json` containing `device=cpu` + `score_axis=diagnostic_cpu`, plus `contest_auth_eval_cuda_work/` directory containing CPU-evaluated artifacts. Parent agent quoted "0.19869 [contest-CUDA T4]" from the FILENAME despite the metadata saying CPU. Paired re-eval revealed true CUDA = 0.2317 — the originally-claimed CUDA score did not exist. This is the "phantom-score directory" anti-pattern: an artifact whose FILENAME makes a claim its CONTENT cannot honor. Per CLAUDE.md "Apples-to-apples evidence discipline" + "Forbidden component-aliasing for baselines" non-negotiables, the filename MUST match the device that produced the contents OR be device-agnostic. Bug-class spread (META-meta 6-7x per CLAUDE.md): 23 substrate trainers + 4 deployment files + canonical helper all hardcoded `_cuda.json`. The canonical helper `gate_auth_eval_call` derives `work_dir` from `output_json.stem`, so a single misleading filename poisoned the work-dir name too. Companion runtime fix in `src/tac/substrates/_shared/smoke_auth_eval_gate.py` adds `_redirect_output_json_to_match_device` which auto-rewrites mismatched filenames at runtime + emits a loud `[phantom-score-fix]` warning so the upstream caller can be migrated. The runtime auto-redirect extincts the actual lie surface immediately; this STRICT preflight gate is the structural sister that refuses NEW hardcoded device tokens in source code so future trainers don't regrow the bug class at a different surface. Detection is AST-aware (string-literal targets only with auth-eval context tokens like `contest_auth_eval` / `auth_eval_result` / `auth_eval_json` / `auth_eval_path` AND `_<device>.json` / `_<device>_work` / `_<device>` suffix). Same-line waiver `# DEVICE_NAMED_DIR_OK:<rationale>` accepts the rare deliberate case (e.g., a CUDA-only benchmark fixture); placeholder `<rationale>` literal rejected. Self-exempt: canonical helper file + this gate's defining file. Path markers excluded: `experiments/results/` (DERIVED_OUTPUT per Catalog #113) + `_intake_` (Catalog #109) + `.omx/oss_export/` + `vendored` + `/tests/` + `test_*` files + `build/lib/` + `reports/raw/`. Sister of Catalog #127 (`check_authoritative_tag_requires_custody_metadata` — per-call-site custody routing) + Catalog #221 (`check_auth_eval_result_artifacts_fail_closed_for_score_claims` — result-artifact fail-closed) + Catalog #226 (`check_trainer_auth_eval_uses_canonical_helper` — caller-must-route-through-canonical-helper). Together they extinct the phantom-score class at FOUR surfaces: per-call-site custody routing (#127) + result-artifact fail-closed (#221) + caller-must-route-through-canonical-helper (#226) + filename-must-match-device (#249). Initial wire-in is WARN-ONLY per CLAUDE.md "Strict-flip atomicity rule" because 23 substrate trainers + 4 deploy files currently hardcode `contest_auth_eval_cuda.json` — strict-flip planned once a sister wave drives caller-side hardcoded filenames to 0. Live count at landing: 32 hardcoded device-named auth-eval paths across ~27 files. Companion FORBIDDEN_PATTERNS entry "Forbidden misleading-directory-name (the phantom-score directory trap)" lands in same commit batch. 22 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_check_249_misleading_device_named_output_dirs.py` covering runtime auto-redirect (preserve agnostic / preserve matched cuda+cpu / rewrite cuda→cpu Z3 anchor / rewrite cpu→cuda / handle mps token / handle unrelated filenames) + STRICT preflight gate (clean repo / flag hardcoded cuda literal in substrate / accept same-line waiver with rationale / reject placeholder waiver / exclude test files / exclude results artifacts / exclude intake clones / strict-mode raises with Catalog #249 + phantom-score message / strict-mode silent on clean / aggregate multi-file violations / recognize cpu reverse lie / recognize work_dir suffix / self-exempt canonical helper / live-repo bounded baseline / orchestrator-callsite warn-only wire-in regression guard). Memory: `feedback_misleading_directory_name_phantom_score_class_permanent_fix_landed_20260515.md`. 272. `check_substrate_distinguishing_feature_integration_contract` — DISTINGUISHING-FEATURE INTEGRATION CONTRACT 2026-05-15 (per operator NON-NEGOTIABLE *"the thing that makes them special and smart is not actually engineered or wired or integrated correctly"*). Bug class anchor: Z3-G1 trained a 1KB SegNet-class CDF (the "smart" distinguishing thing) but the archive's `hyperprior_weights_int8` slot is `b""` — the smart thing was engineered, never wired. Smoke score == Z3 v2 baseline to 5 decimals (empirical proof at fc-01KRPKCXARWP7NBGJCXB2P9QEP). This is the per-substrate-distinguishing-feature variant of Catalog #220 (operational mechanism) and #139 (no-op detector). Where #220 catches "lane added bytes but no operational mechanism declared" and #139 catches "some byte mutation changes output (generic)", #272 catches "the bytes claimed as DISTINGUISHING are actually consumed by inflate (specific)". Refuses substrate L2+ lanes in `.omx/state/lane_registry.json` that do NOT declare all 4 contract fields (`distinguishing_feature_name` / `distinguishing_bytes_path` / `inflate_consumer_function` / `byte_mutation_smoke_passes`). Acceptance cascade: (a) all 4 fields present + `byte_mutation_smoke_passes` evidence == True; (b) `research_only=true` (top-level field OR notes/evidence token); (c) `lane_class=substrate_engineering` (top-level field OR notes); (d) same-line waiver `# DISTINGUISHING_FEATURE_CONTRACT_OK:<rationale>` in lane notes / evidence (placeholder `<rationale>` / `<reason>` literal rejected). Companion runtime helper `tools/verify_distinguishing_feature_byte_mutation.py` (~360 LOC) is the canonical proof generator: mutates one byte at every declared distinguishing offset, re-runs `inflate.sh` source-vs-mutated, compares output frames byte-for-byte, emits `experiments/results/<lane>/distinguishing_feature_byte_mutation_proof.json` with per-section verdict (PASSED / FAILED / INFRASTRUCTURE_ERROR; rc 0 / 1 / 2). Companion runtime hardening in `tools/subagent_commit_serializer.py`: (a) every event now logs `expected_content_sha256_present` boolean + `expected_content_sha256_file_count` integer so the gate has structured evidence; (b) high-risk files (`src/tac/preflight.py`, `CLAUDE.md`) flip opt-in → opt-out — committing them WITHOUT `--expected-content-sha256` returns rc=5 + writes `outcome=high_risk_file_missing_expected_content_sha` log row. Cutoff filter `_CHECK_289_DISCIPLINE_CUTOFF_UTC = "2026-05-15T22:00:00Z"` exempts pre-cutoff legacy events per CLAUDE.md "Strict-flip atomicity rule"; legacy schema events without `expected_content_sha256_present` field also exempt (pre-GAP-5 logging). 293. `check_cathedral_literature_anchors_have_source_scope` — Cathedral/autopilot source-fidelity self-protection 2026-05-16. Refuses any canonical substrate composition row or serialized Pareto row with a non-empty `literature_anchor` unless it also carries non-placeholder `source_supports`, `paper_claim_scope`, `pact_must_prove`, and `decode_complexity_evidence`. Bug class: a Cathedral row can cite Ballé / Atick-Redlich / Rao-Ballard / DreamerV3 / world-model / MDL/IB papers and carry a predicted score band while silently dropping the boundary between what the paper actually supports and what Pact must prove on byte-closed contest archives. The source-scope fields keep literature anchors as hypothesis provenance, not promotion evidence. STRICT-from-byte-one because `src/tac/optimization/substrate_composition_matrix.py` already carries the complete source-scope contract for all current anchored rows and serialized Pareto rows preserve the fields. Sister of Catalog #291/#292 (assumption/fidelity review surfaces), Catalog #125 (solver wire-in), and Catalog #233 (promotion evidence discipline). 295. `check_submission_inflate_works_with_empty_pythonpath` — SUBMISSION INFLATE.PY WORKS WITH EMPTY PYTHONPATH 2026-05-16 self-protection per NSCS06 v5 Modal dispatch failure anchor `fc-01KRQMAQ7V41AFYMJH5HRK9P10` (`submissions/nscs06_carmack_hotz_strip_everything/inflate.py` used PYTHONPATH-shim pattern `sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE / "src"))` then `from tac.substrates...` which worked locally but FAILED on Modal worker because `src/tac/...` was never vendored into the submission directory). Refuses any `submissions/*/inflate.py` (excluding `submissions/exact_current/` per CLAUDE.md mutation frontier; excluding `_intake_` vendored clones) that EITHER (a) imports `from tac.*` / `import tac.*` WITHOUT a sibling `tac/` package vendored alongside (the NSCS06 v5 bug class via implicit working-tree dependency), OR (b) calls `sys.path.insert(...)` pointing OUTSIDE the submission directory tree (e.g. `HERE.parent / "sibling_sub" / "src"` or unresolved variable expression). Acceptance cascades: (1) no `sys.path.insert` + no `from tac.*` imports = canonical simple inflate; (2) `sys.path.insert(HERE / "src")` + no tac imports = NSCS06-v6-style vendored non-tac codec alongside (canonical pattern @ commit `90bca47ff`); (3) `sys.path.insert(HERE / "src")` + `from tac.*` AND vendored `src/tac/__init__.py` alongside = explicit-vendor accept; (4) `sys.path.insert(HERE)` + `from tac.*` AND vendored `tac/__init__.py` directly alongside = explicit-vendor accept; (5) one-level variable indirection (`SRC_DIR = HERE / "src"` then `sys.path.insert(0, str(SRC_DIR))`) traced by AST module-level binding walker; (6) same-line `# SUBMISSION_PYTHONPATH_SHIM_OK:<rationale>` waiver on the `sys.path.insert(...)` line (placeholder `<rationale>` / `<reason>` literals rejected so the gate's docstring example cannot self-waive). Per CLAUDE.md HNeRV parity discipline lesson 9 (Runtime closure — dependency closure failures including local paths and hidden sidecars are runtime blockers); per the 8th forbidden pattern (research-substrate trap — a submission that cannot be run from an empty PYTHONPATH on the Modal worker is by construction not self-contained). Sister of Catalog #205 (`check_inflate_py_uses_canonical_select_inflate_device` — same `submissions/*/inflate.py` scan surface; #205 catches inline device-fork; #295 catches PYTHONPATH-shim self-containment violations). **STRICT-FLIPPED 2026-05-16** by SISTER-BACKFILL-WAVE per CLAUDE.md "Strict-flip atomicity rule" — live count at landing: 0 (driven from 6 → 0 in same commit batch via the 4-priority backfill below). Backfill mapping per `.omx/research/submission_inflate_pythonpath_shim_audit_20260516.md` 3-column inventory: **P1** `nscs01_nullspace_split_renderer` adopted the NSCS01-style fail-closed-at-runtime waiver path explicitly named in the gate's own docstring (the checked-in shim raises `RuntimeError` before any `sys.path.insert` reaches the operator's working tree; the trainer-emitted `submission_dir/inflate.py` is GENUINELY self-contained via `_write_runtime` already-existing vendoring of `src/tac/substrates/nscs01_nullspace_split_renderer/` + `src/tac/substrates/_shared/inflate_runtime.py` into `submission_dir/src/tac/...`). **P2a** `apogee_v2` adopted the NSCS06-v6 vendor-the-codec pattern (vendored `src/tac/water_filling_codec_v2.py` + `src/tac/arithmetic_qint_codec.py` into `submissions/apogee_v2/src/tac/__init__.py` + 2 modules; encode-side `_EXP_MAX/_EXP_MIN/QINT_LEVELS/WaterFillError/water_fill_bit_budget` constants/stubs inlined so the vendored copy imports cleanly on a worker with NO `tac.block_fp_codec` / `tac.water_filling_codec` present; verified empirically by `PYTHONPATH="" python -c "...decode_omega_w_v2..."` from cwd=/tmp). **P2b** `magic_codec_pr106_r2` adopted same-line `# SUBMISSION_PYTHONPATH_SHIM_OK` waivers on the 2 function-scoped lazy `from tac.packet_compiler.*` imports (the module's docstring explicitly designates it as research-only / non-promotable / requires sibling submission + tac runtime co-mounted via LAB dispatch flow). **P3** `pr106_lrl1_sidechannel` + `pr106_stacked` + `pr106_yshift_sidechannel` adopted same-line `# SUBMISSION_PYTHONPATH_SHIM_OK` waivers on their `sys.path.insert(HERE.parent / "apogee_intN" / "src")` / `HERE.parent / "pr106_latent_sidecar" / "src"` lines documenting sibling-submission shipping requirements per audit Priority 4 option (b). Gate-side hardening also landed in the same commit batch: the no-`sys.path.insert`-but-`from tac.*`-imports branch in `_scan_inflate_for_pythonpath_shim_self_contained` now respects same-line waivers consistent with the violation message's "OR add same-line `# SUBMISSION_PYTHONPATH_SHIM_OK:<rationale>` waiver" advice (previously a code-vs-message gap where the gate refused waivers in this code path). 23 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_check_295_submission_inflate_empty_pythonpath.py` covering live-repo regression guard (≤20 ceiling), positive (bare tac import without sys.path / sys.path.insert + tac import no vendor / sys.path.insert parent traversal), negative (no sys.path + no tac / NSCS06-v6 vendored alongside / pr106-style vendored codec no tac / tac vendored in src subdir / tac vendored directly / one-level indirection), waiver semantics (rationale accepted / `<rationale>` + `<reason>` placeholders rejected), scope (exact_current exempt / intake clones exempt / no submissions dir empty), edge cases (malformed Python tolerated / docstring mention not flagged), strict-mode (raises with Catalog #295 message / silent on clean), multi-file aggregation, orchestrator-callsite warn-only wire-in regression guard, and live-repo NSCS01-remains-flagged regression guard. Bug class: per the 9-dim checklist directive, every substrate landing AND every stack-of-stacks composition memo must document evidence across ALL 9 dimensions: (1) UNIQUENESS (class-shift not within-class); (2) BEAUTY + ELEGANCE (PR101-style 30-sec-reviewable); (3) DISTINCTNESS (explicitly different from sisters); (4) RIGOR (premise verification + adversarial review + assumption classification + empirical anchor); (5) OPTIMIZATION PER TECHNIQUE (substrate-optimal engineering — covered by sister Catalog #290); (6) STACK-OF-STACKS-COMPOSABILITY (orthogonal axes + additive ΔS); (7) DETERMINISTIC REPRODUCIBILITY (byte-stable + seed-pinned); (8) EXTREME OPTIMIZATION + PERFORMANCE; (9) OPTIMAL MINIMAL CONTEST SCORE. 280. `check_dispatch_protocol_unresolved_recipe_blocks_unless_paired_waiver` — Codex review bklem3v5j HIGH F2 self-protection 2026-05-15. Refuses any state of `tools/canonical_dispatch_optimization_protocol.py` where the unresolved-recipe branch of `_verify_tier2` / `_verify_tier3` sets per-signal True (vacuous PASS) without paired-env discipline (`--allow-no-recipe-advisory-mode` AND `--no-recipe-rationale <text>` per Catalog #199 sister). Bug class anchor: codex review bklem3v5j HIGH F2 caught that when `recipe_path is None` the prior implementation marked every Tier 2 recipe-field check `True` (vacuous PASS) which made strict callers silently approve dispatch with NO recipe-side coverage; Tier 3 recipe-vs-trainer-state divergence (the Z3 v2 / Z4 / Z5 bug class anchor; Catalog #240) had the same pattern. A strict CLI / preflight invocation with no recipe could therefore return `overall_pass=True` while skipping `min_vram_gb` / `target_modes` / `video_input_strategy` / `canary_status` / hardware-routing / research-only checks. Companion runtime fix (a) added paired-env kwargs `allow_no_recipe_advisory_mode: bool = False` + `no_recipe_rationale: str | None = None` to `_verify_tier2` / `_verify_tier3` / `verify_dispatch_protocol_complete`; (b) changed the unresolved-recipe branches to BLOCK every Tier 2/3 signal (default fail-closed); (c) added CLI flags `--allow-no-recipe-advisory-mode` + `--no-recipe-rationale <text>` with paired-env validator that raises `SystemExit(11)` on bare opt-in without rationale; (d) per codex's explicit recommendation, even when paired advisory-mode is opted into, `--strict` STILL exits nonzero (the umbrella retains an explicit advisory-mode blocker so advisory != pass). Empirical receipts: `--strict` on a recipe-less invocation → rc=1 (was rc=0 before fix); bare `--allow-no-recipe-advisory-mode` (no rationale) → rc=11; paired `--allow-no-recipe-advisory-mode --no-recipe-rationale <text>` + `--strict` → rc=1 (advisory != pass). File-level `# DISPATCH_PROTOCOL_UNRESOLVED_RECIPE_VACUOUS_OK:<rationale>` waiver in the file's first 80 lines accepts the rare deliberate case (placeholder `<rationale>` / `<reason>` literal rejected). STRICT-from-byte-one per CLAUDE.md "Bugs must be permanently fixed AND self-protected against" + "Strict-flip atomicity rule" non-negotiables — live count at landing: 0 (the F2 fix lands in the same commit batch). 14 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_check_280_dispatch_protocol_unresolved_recipe_blocks.py` covering live-repo regression guard, positive (vacuous-True F2 regression flagged / missing paired-env kwargs flagged / missing blocker-message tokens flagged), negative (canonical fix accepted), waiver semantics (rationale accepted / placeholder rejected), strict-mode raises with Catalog #280, edge cases (missing target file silent), orchestrator strict=True wire-in regression guard, AND 3 CLI subprocess regression tests per codex's explicit recommendations: (i) `--strict` recipe-less exits nonzero, (ii) bare advisory opt-in without rationale rejected rc=11, (iii) paired advisory opt-in + `--strict` STILL nonzero (advisory != pass). 314. `check_no_subagent_files_touched_absorption_in_bare_commits` — COMMIT-SWAP-INVESTIGATION self-protection 2026-05-16 (per CLAUDE.md "Bugs must be permanently fixed AND self-protected against" non-negotiable + 2 empirical anchors today: (a) WAVE-D 2c957c31e forensic finding 2026-05-15 (CODEX-FIX-WAVE absorbed DISPATCH-OPTIMIZATION's preflight.py + CLAUDE.md edits via DROP-FLAG-AND-RETRY pattern; closed by Catalog #289); (b) STC v2 FIX 2026-05-16 (commits `89d89c27e` "Harden L5 sideinfo and dispatch probe gates" + `c09c6e1c8` "Preserve probe outcomes landing memo" + `5562afc3c` "Preserve L5 v2 TT5L audit signal" absorbed STC v2 FIX subagent's edits to `src/tac/preflight.py` + `src/tac/tests/test_check_152_modal_mounted_input_extension.py` + `CLAUDE.md` + 3 driver scripts (`scripts/remote_lane_substrate_stc_v2.sh` / `a1_plus_lapose` / `a1_plus_wavelet_residual`) BEFORE STC v2 FIX's canonical serializer call ran; Catalog #157 `--expected-content-sha256` did NOT fire rc=4 because the absorbed content IS the working-tree state at lock-acquire time)). Refuses bare commits whose file list intersects an in-flight subagent's declared `files_touched` checkpoint within the preceding 60-minute window (excluding common-shared exempt files). Root cause: the operator's `/commit` slash command (commit-commands plugin at `~/.claude/plugins/marketplaces/claude-plugins-official/plugins/commit-commands/commands/commit.md`) does bare `git add` + `git commit` directly (NOT through `tools/subagent_commit_serializer.py`). When invoked while a sister subagent has uncommitted edits in the shared working tree, the bare `git add` packages whatever the LLM thinks is relevant — which can include the sister's still-in-flight edits. The resulting commit carries a terse subject like "Preserve probe outcomes landing memo" or "Harden L5 sideinfo and dispatch probe gates" and the sister's work is silently attributed to the wrong commit body. WHY existing protections did not extinct this: Catalog #117 (subagent commit serializer must be used) IS detecting the bare commits (12 violations in last 50 today, including all 3 absorption commits) but is wired warn-only per legacy backlog. It DETECTS but does NOT BLOCK; #157 / #174 / #216 / #289 (--expected-content-sha256 family) protect against sister-edits-during-lock-wait + drop-flag-and-retry, not bare-commit-absorbs-files-before-serializer; #230 (bulk-rewrite ownership map) covers the COMMIT-MESSAGE surface; not the bare-commit-with-terse-subject case; #302 (sister subagent scope overlap via checkpoint JSONL) covers the SIMULTANEOUS-EDIT surface (2+ subagents both editing same files); not the BARE-COMMIT-ABSORBS-IN-FLIGHT-FILES case. Catalog #314 closes the absorption-pattern surface as a dedicated detector scoped to the empirical fingerprint: a bare commit (not in serializer log, no absorption-specific waiver) containing files declared as `files_touched` by an in-flight subagent (status=in_progress) within a ~60-minute window preceding the commit timestamp. The detection is POST-HOC (the commit already landed). This is by design: we cannot block the `/commit` slash command from within preflight because preflight runs before commit, not before edit. The structural value is operator-facing alert + machine-readable evidence for the next subagent wave so absorption is detected within the same session rather than discovered weeks later when the operator wonders "why did THIS commit include files I never asked about?". Acceptance: (a) commit went through `tools/subagent_commit_serializer.py` (matched by serializer log via prefix-aware sha lookup mirroring Catalog #117 / #235 semantics); (b) commit message body carries `# ABSORPTION_PATTERN_OK:<rationale>` waiver (placeholder `<rationale>` / `<reason>` literals rejected; rationale must be ≥4 chars). Exempt files (commonly multi-subagent state): `.omx/state/lane_registry.json` / `.omx/state/lane_maturity_audit.log` / `.omx/state/modal_call_id_ledger.jsonl` / `.omx/state/active_lane_dispatch_claims.md` / `.omx/state/commit-serializer.log` / `.omx/state/catalog-claim.log` / `.omx/state/subagent_progress.jsonl` / `.omx/state/continual_learning_posterior.jsonl` / `.omx/state/cost_band_posterior.jsonl` / `.omx/state/next_catalog_number.txt` / `.omx/state/probe_outcomes.jsonl` / `MEMORY.md`. Sister of Catalog #117 (last-50 commit serializer usage gate; #314 is the per-absorption-fingerprint complement that catches the bug class even when #117 has legacy backlog), Catalog #157 / #174 / #216 / #289 (commit-swap protection family), Catalog #230 (bulk-rewrite ownership map; commit-message surface), Catalog #302 (sister subagent scope overlap via checkpoint JSONL; edit-time-checkpoint surface). Together they extinct the multi-subagent edit/commit collision class at SEVEN surfaces: edit-time-checkpoint (#302) + edit-time-bulk-op (#230) + commit-time-pre-pre-lock (#157) + commit-time-staged (#216) + commit-time-lock-arbitration (#117) + post-resolution-residual-marker (#248) + bare-commit-absorbs-in-flight-files (#314). Search cascade per phase: (i) inside `submissions/<sub>/`; (ii) inside `experiments/results/<sub>*/`; (iii) inside `reports/pr_pre_submission/` (Phase 6 convention); most-recent-mtime wins. Acceptance cascade: (a) all 4 sidecars present + clean (the canonical default-path); (b) same-line `# PR_SUBMISSION_NO_CANONICAL_COMPLIANCE_OK:<rationale>` waiver in README.md or PR_BODY*.md first 30 lines (non-placeholder rationale ≥4 chars; placeholder `<rationale>` / `<reason>` literals rejected per Catalog #287 sister discipline so the gate's docstring example cannot self-waive); (c) submission is not PR-facing (no PR_BODY*.md AND README.md lacks PR-facing sentinel tokens). Bug class anchor: 2026-05-19 PR101 submission_dir was hand-edited across 4 sister subagents (Slot K + L + M + J) over ~3h wall-clock with no canonical pipeline structurally enforcing the 4-verdict chain. Per Phase 1 spec memo §1 Why: "6+ phases × multiple iterations × manual hand-editing per surface = the canonical anti-pattern." The structural extinction at this gate prevents future PR111+ submissions from shipping without all 4 canonical verdicts being present + clean. Per CLAUDE.md "Operator gates must be wired and used" non-negotiable: every missing canonical verdict raises with a named blocker citing WHICH verdict is missing AND the canonical CLI command to generate it (operator-actionable remediation hint per Catalog #270 sister discipline). Initial wire-in is **WARN-ONLY** per CLAUDE.md "Strict-flip atomicity rule" + Phase 1 spec memo §3 Phase 8 acceptance contract; strict-flip pending Phase 10 PR101 baseline migration + first NEW submission both PACKET-CLEAN.
