367. `check_substrate_inflate_emits_expected_frame_count_or_fail_closed` — INFLATE RAW BYTES FAIL-OPEN extension to Catalog #146 self-protection 2026-05-26 per FIX-WAVE-CASCADE-C-PRIME-ALL-BUGS-PERMANENT-FIX-AND-SELF-PROTECT directive + CLAUDE.md "Bugs must be permanently fixed AND self-protected against" non-negotiable + Cascade C' WAVE-3 empirical anchor (commit `39e1db080` + fix commits `5bcb53070` + `d0c4517ea`). Sister of Catalog #146 at the FRAME-EMISSION-COUNT sub-surface; refuses inflate.py files that emit raw bytes AND reference the contest output contract but lack a fail-closed check on raw_bytes / frame_count / byte_count. Bug class anchor: Cascade C' WAVE-3 Modal T4 dispatch `fc-01KSKB4B30DCYTCP883XYV5BNV` 2026-05-26 rc=0 elapsed=11.62s with stage 1-6 SUCCESS but stage 7 contest_auth_eval crashed with `RuntimeError: [inflate] WRONG-SIZE .raw file(s): 0.raw=707788800B (expected 3662409600B). Each must be 3,662,409,600 bytes (1164x874x1200x3). Likely truncated mid-decode.` Empirical ratio: 707788800 / 3662409600 = 19.33%. Root cause: WAVE-3 inflate.py at commit `aaf0b1eb6` emitted 1200 frames at 384×512×3 resolution (1200 × 384 × 512 × 3 = 707,788,800) instead of 1200 frames at 1164×874×3 (contest output resolution). Fix landed Cascade C' commits `5bcb53070` + `d0c4517ea`; current inflate.py at HEAD has `CONTEST_OUT_H=874` + `CONTEST_OUT_W=1164` + `CONTEST_NUM_FRAMES=1200` + `CONTEST_RAW_BYTES = CONTEST_OUT_W * CONTEST_OUT_H * CONTEST_NUM_FRAMES * 3` + fail-closed `if raw_bytes != CONTEST_RAW_BYTES: raise AssertionError(...)`. This META-class self-protect catches ANY future regression where a substrate inflate.py emits raw bytes that don't match the contest 1164×874×1200×3 = 3,662,409,600 byte contract. Detection: scans `submissions/*/inflate.py` (excluding `submissions/exact_current/` per CLAUDE.md mutation frontier) + `src/tac/substrates/*/inflate.py` (120 files total) for files that emit raw bytes (`.raw` / `raw_bytes` / `with_suffix` tokens) AND reference the contest output contract (`CONTEST_RAW_BYTES` / `CONTEST_NUM_FRAMES` / `CONTEST_OUT_H` / `CONTEST_OUT_W` / `1164` / `3662409600` / `3_662_409_600` tokens); refuses files lacking ANY fail-closed token (`raise AssertionError` / `raise ValueError` / `raise RuntimeError` / `assert raw_bytes` / `!= CONTEST_RAW_BYTES` / `wrong-size` / `WRONG-SIZE` / etc.). Acceptance: (a) fail-closed token present in file; (b) file-level `# INFLATE_FRAME_COUNT_FAIL_OPEN_OK:<rationale>` waiver anywhere in file (rationale ≥4 chars; placeholder rejected per Catalog #287). Initial wire-in is **WARN-ONLY** per CLAUDE.md "Strict-flip atomicity rule" — live count at landing: 5 pre-existing inflate.py files (`pr101_lc_v2_clone` / `pr95_lora_dora` / `sabor_boundary_only_renderer` / `factorized_hnerv_v1` / `pr103_pr106_final_runtime` — all L1 scaffolds or submitted-runtime variants that predate Catalog #367). Strict-flip pending operator-routed backfill sweep that EITHER adds the canonical fail-closed check OR adds an explicit waiver per file. Sister of Catalog #146 (contest-compliant template at design surface; #367 is the runtime-effect surface) + Catalog #205 (canonical select_inflate_device) + Catalog #220 (L1+ scaffold operational mechanism) + Catalog #272 (distinguishing-feature integration contract) + Catalog #295 (PYTHONPATH self-containment). Together they extinct the inflate-emits-wrong-bytes bug class at FIVE surfaces: contract template (#146) + device selection (#205) + operational mechanism (#220) + distinguishing-feature contract (#272) + PYTHONPATH self-containment (#295) + raw-bytes fail-closed check (#367 — THIS gate). 17 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_check_365_366_367_cascade_c_prime_bug_class_extinction.py`. 6-hook wire-in per Catalog #125: hooks N/A (defensive validator gate); hook #4 cathedral autopilot dispatch = ACTIVE (prevents future Modal dispatches from silently emitting wrong-sized raw bytes and crashing contest_auth_eval WRONG-SIZE post-train, wasting paid GPU spend); hook #6 probe-disambiguator = ACTIVE (the canonical fail-closed check IS the disambiguator between correctly-sized contest raw output vs resolution/frame-count mismatch). Retroactive sweep per Catalog #348: `.omx/research/retroactive_sweep_for_catalog_367_20260527T002931Z.md`. Memory: `feedback_fix_wave_cascade_c_prime_all_bugs_permanent_fix_and_self_protect_against_landed_20260526.md`. 384. `check_score_aware_run_has_nonzero_scorer_objective_weights` — OBJECTIVE-STARVATION bug class 2026-06-09 per operator NON-NEGOTIABLE (anti-objective-starvation half of the name-laundering + objective-starvation pair; sister Deliverable 1 = the vehicle fidelity manifest `tac.substrates._shared.vehicle_fidelity_manifest` which extincts the orthogonal NAME-LAUNDERING bug class at the docstring surface). **Empirical anchor**: SNeRV's faithful official-MFU/HFR/TUB renderer at ep22399 scored `avg_segnet_dist = 0.711` with `observed_segnet_distillation_weight = None` ([macOS-MLX research-signal] per `.omx/research/snerv_all_vehicles_fidelity_review_vs_evaluate_py_20260609.md`) because the shared MLX harness `src/tac/substrates/snerv_inverse_steg_carrier/mlx_native_train_export.py` declares `segnet_distillation_weight: float = 0.0` + `pose_distillation_weight: float = 0.0` as defaults — "score-aware in name, recon-MSE in gradient." MSE's minimizer is the conditional mean → blurry mean-field that collapses SegNet argmax to ~one class; with the scorer objective weights at 0.0 the run starves the only authority that moves `100*d_seg + sqrt(10*d_pose)`. **Detection** delegates to canonical helper `tac.substrates._shared.score_aware_objective_weight_audit.audit_score_aware_objective_weights`. Scan surface: every `experiments/train_substrate_*.py` + every substrate `score_aware_loss.py` + every substrate `mlx_native_train_export.py`. **Rule chain** (falling-rule list): a file VIOLATES when it (1) CLAIMS score-aware / PR95-style / scorer-aware (token match) AND (2) defaults a SegNet (`segnet_distillation_weight` / `segnet_direct_live_distillation_weight`) OR PoseNet (`pose_distillation_weight` / `pose_direct_live_distillation_weight`) objective weight to `0.0` / `None` AND (3) provides NONE of: (a) explicit NONZERO SegNet AND PoseNet weights; (b) an explicit `score_aware=false` / `scoreaware=false` (or `research_only` / `dispatch_enabled=false`) opt-out — a recon-only / research / non-dispatch scaffold makes no score-aware-trained claim; (c) same-line `# SCORE_AWARE_OBJECTIVE_WEIGHTS_OK:<rationale>` waiver (placeholder `<rationale>` / `<reason>` literals rejected per Catalog #287 sister discipline so the gate's docstring example cannot self-waive). **Initial wire-in is WARN-ONLY** per CLAUDE.md "Strict-flip atomicity rule" because the live count is 3 (backfill list: `experiments/train_substrate_hi_nerv_mlx_local.py` + `experiments/train_substrate_time_traveler_l5_z7_mamba2_mlx_local.py` + `src/tac/substrates/snerv_inverse_steg_carrier/mlx_native_train_export.py` — all empirically-confirmed objective-starvation carriers from the 20260609 fidelity audits). Strict-flip planned after those 3 set explicit nonzero SegNet+PoseNet weights OR declare an opt-out. **Catalog #299 quota brake decision**: NEW gate (NOT a scope extension) because the TRAINED-OBJECTIVE-WEIGHT surface is structurally distinct from any existing gate; current count 384 under 400 quota. Sister of the vehicle fidelity manifest (Deliverable 1; docstring-surface NAME-LAUNDERING) + Catalog #287 (placeholder-rationale rejection) + Catalog #176 (META-meta: STRICT-callsite-has-CLAUDE.md-row — THIS entry satisfies that META gate; gate is WARN-ONLY so the callsite is not yet STRICT) + Catalog #185 (META-meta-meta: live count noted = 3, gate WARN-ONLY) + Catalog #324 (post-training Tier-C validation; sister recipe-emit surface). **6-hook wire-in declaration** per Catalog #125: hook #1 sensitivity-map = N/A (defensive validator gate); hook #2 Pareto constraint = N/A; hook #3 bit-allocator = N/A; hook #4 cathedral autopilot dispatch = **ACTIVE** (prevents future score-aware-named runs from silently optimizing recon-MSE-only and wasting MLX/paid spend on a mean-field that cannot move the contest score); hook #5 continual-learning posterior = N/A (the audit memo IS the empirical anchor; no new canonical equation); hook #6 probe-disambiguator = **ACTIVE** (the explicit nonzero weight vs the `score_aware=false` opt-out IS the disambiguator between a genuine score-aware run and a recon-only one). Mission contribution per Catalog #300: `frontier_breaking_enabler` (the bug class silently starved the SNeRV/HiNeRV/Z7 carriers of the scorer gradient — fixing it unblocks the spectral-bias fixes those carriers need to break the d_seg≈0.5 ceiling). 371. `check_no_orphan_auto_trigger_stub_with_satisfied_condition` — STUB-AUDIT-AND-FIX wave 2026-05-27 (operator directive "fix all stubs and continue iterating and optimizing and auditing") + CLAUDE.md "Results must become system intelligence" (a no-op consumer of landed evidence is the orphan-work failure mode) + "Bugs must be permanently fixed AND self-protected against" non-negotiable. **Bug class anchor**: `tac.canonical_equations.registry.auto_recalibrate_from_continual_learning_posterior` shipped as a no-op stub (`equations_recalibrated=0,  # stub; auto-refit comes in a follow-on landing`) that returned WITHOUT refitting even when its OWN documented trigger condition (`when_3+_new_empirical_anchors_in_domain`) was ALREADY satisfied. Empirical receipt: 11 of 64 canonical equations carried stale `predicted_vs_empirical_residual` summaries that no longer matched their own landed `EmpiricalAnchor` rows (including stale NaN sentinels + synthetic `count`/`max_abs_residual`/`amplification_factor` keys polluting `is_well_calibrated`), and the stub no-op'd on all of them. canonical equation #2 (`hinton_kl_distill_enables_qat_catalyst_composition_savings_v1`) held 3 anchors falsifying the closed-form alpha=0.15 lift toward an empirical ~0 (2nd anchor IMPLEMENTATION_LEVEL_FALSIFIED catalyst-NOT-improving-over-baseline; 3rd PARTIAL_CONFIRMATION delta=0.0055 not 0.15), yet its summary + `last_calibration_utc` never moved because the recalibrator no-op'd even with its trigger satisfied. **The fix** (companion landing same commit batch): the recalibrator now, for every equation whose `next_recalibration_trigger` is `when_3+_new_empirical_anchors_in_domain` AND anchor count >= 3, re-derives `predicted_vs_empirical_residual` directly from the equation's own landed anchors (latest-wins per `measurement_method` axis, mirroring `with_new_anchor`), and if the recomputed map differs from the stored map, appends a canonical `EVENT_RECALIBRATED` row (bumping `last_calibration_utc`). NOT a Catalog #287 violation: no anchor synthesized + no empirical_output fabricated — only the residual SUMMARY is re-derived from anchors that already landed via signed `update_equation_with_empirical_anchor` calls. Idempotent (2nd run recalibrates 0). Equations on other triggers (`when_residual_drift_exceeds_2x` / `when_operator_invokes` / `never_auto`) reported but NOT auto-refit. **This gate** refuses re-introduction of the orphan-auto-trigger-stub: an `auto_recalibrate_*` function that hardcodes `equations_recalibrated=0` / carries the follow-on-stub marker WITHOUT wiring the canonical `EVENT_RECALIBRATED` emission path. Acceptance: (a) the recalibrator references `EVENT_RECALIBRATED` (refit emission wired); OR (b) the no-op marker line carries a same-line `# DEFERRED_STUB_OK:<reactivation-criteria>` waiver with a substantive non-placeholder criterion (>= 4 chars; `<rationale>` / `<reason>` / `<reactivation-criteria>` placeholders rejected per Catalog #287 sister discipline). STRICT-from-byte-one per CLAUDE.md "Strict-flip atomicity rule" — the working recalibrator lands in the same commit batch driving live count to 0. 363. `check_council_deliberation_has_empirical_verification_status` — RECURSIVE-SELF-REFLECTION PROTOCOL self-protection 2026-05-26 (per operator NON-NEGOTIABLE directive 2026-05-26 verbatim *"the grand council is providing valuable information but perhaps the grand council itself must be instructed to deliberate and self reflect recursively"*). Lifts the CLAUDE.md "Recursive adversarial review protocol — close paths" 3-clean-pass counter from the training-code review surface to the **council deliberation surface**: every T2+ council deliberation MUST recursively self-reflect on its OWN deliberation process, classifying each per-member surfaced assumption (already required by Catalog #292) into the canonical 4-value `empirical_verification_status` taxonomy (`VERIFIED_VIA_SOURCE_INSPECTION` / `VERIFIED_VIA_EMPIRICAL_ANCHOR` / `INFERRED_FROM_DOMAIN_LITERATURE` / `ASSUMED_AWAITING_VERIFICATION`). Verdicts depending on `INFERRED` / `ASSUMED`-class assumptions must EITHER empirically verify before landing OR downgrade verdict-status to `PROVISIONAL-PENDING-VERIFICATION`. **Empirical receipts (3+ instances within <2h on 2026-05-26)**: (1) T3 grand council `7d04474cb` M3 RULED-OUT empirically falsified by sister TIER1-T3-OP1-OP4 source-inspection `5b87fae77` (Z6 uses MLX AdamW not stateless SGD-with-EMA as the council assumed); (2) T3 council M2 ~0.7-0.9 α dominance prediction empirically falsified by sister TIER1-T3-OP2-OP3 Carmack smoke `05c07aa40` (canonical Kahan-EMA shadow wrapper provides 0× empirical mitigation at Z6 L2 fp32 1000ep); (3) my own n=2 super-linear α∝epochs^1.45 extrapolation empirically falsified by sister DRIFT-VS-DEPTH-CHAR-D-Z6 `60a9de751` (5-anchor fit yielding α=0.47 sub-linear saturating at ~2000ep); (4) K=COIN++ 5e-3 drift claim earlier 2026-05-26 empirically falsified by sister R1''-K independent verification commits leading to `2d59283d4` (actual O(1e-2) abs / O(1e-3) rel). All 4 verdicts achieved quorum-met + Catalog #346 complete=True + Catalog #292 assumption-statement-surfacing satisfied. **The structural blindness recurs at the sister surface**: existing gates enforce that assumptions ARE surfaced + classified HARD-EARNED-vs-CARGO-CULTED, but NOT that each surfaced assumption carries explicit `empirical_verification_status` AND that verdicts depending on `INFERRED` / `ASSUMED`-class assumptions are gated. **3-clean-pass counter discipline** (lifted from training-code Recursive adversarial review protocol): Round 1 (topic deliberation; each per-member assumption carries explicit `empirical_verification_status` field) → Round 2 (self-reflection; council SELF-REFLECTS on Round 1; re-classifies each assumption's status after dedicated verification cycle attempt; emits `council_self_reflection_round_N` canonical posterior anchor) → Round 3 (resolution; material unverified assumptions trigger empirical verification before landing OR verdict-status downgrade to `PROVISIONAL-PENDING-VERIFICATION` OR `ESCALATE_TO_OPERATOR` per Catalog #300). **Cycle bounds** (per R12-D meta-finding lens-coverage): `MAX_SELF_REFLECTION_ROUNDS = 5`; SEAL when 3 consecutive rounds produce zero material unverified-assumption findings. **Required at T2+ deliberations**; T1 working-group recommendations are exempt (T1 findings feed downstream T2/T3 which inherit the discipline). Acceptance: (a) post-2026-05-26 council memos carry the canonical 4-value taxonomy + Round 2/3 discipline tokens; (b) same-line `# COUNCIL_EMPIRICAL_VERIFICATION_STATUS_WAIVED:<rationale>` waiver with non-placeholder rationale (≥4 chars; placeholder literals rejected per Catalog #287 sister discipline). WARN-ONLY at landing per "Strict-flip atomicity rule"; strict-flip planned after backfill brings live count to 0. **Canonical surfaces**: `tac.council_continual_learning.EmpiricalVerificationStatus` (4-value sentinel constants) + `AssumptionEmpiricalVerification` (frozen dataclass) + `classify_assumption_verification_status_from_evidence` / `extract_unverified_assumptions` / `verdict_status_requires_provisional_marker` / `query_self_reflection_history_for_deliberation` canonical helpers. Sister of Catalog #292 (per-deliberation assumption-statement-surfacing axis; #363 enforces the sister per-assumption empirical-verification-status axis at a distinct sub-surface) + Catalog #291 (META-ASSUMPTION cadence — session-level cousin) + Catalog #300 (v2 frontmatter — canonical posterior schema; #363 records propagate via the existing `council_assumption_adversary_verdict` field structure extended with the 4-value taxonomy) + Catalog #340 / #314 (PREVENT vs DETECT sister gate pattern precedent that #363 lifts to the council-deliberation surface) + Catalog #346 (canonical roster validation — structurally distinct axis) + Catalog #287 (placeholder-rationale rejection sister discipline) + Catalog #344 (canonical equations registry — Round 2 verification path may register the verified assumption as a canonical equation when generalizable). 361. `check_modal_artifact_filter_preserves_submission_dir` — OVERNIGHT-GG META-fix self-protection 2026-05-21 (per CLAUDE.md "Bugs must be permanently fixed AND self-protected against" non-negotiable + OVERNIGHT-CC `99d06f967` IMPLEMENTATION-LEVEL falsification per Catalog #307). Refuses any state of `experiments/modal_train_lane.py` where the artifact harvester applies `mtime_floor` to files under `output/submission/` without the canonical `under_submission` bypass. **Bug class anchor**: OVERNIGHT-CC 99d06f967 4 paired DP1 auth_eval Modal dispatches (fc-01KS5KW2EZBYYKV8DP1H8T0X2D + 3 sister CPU/CUDA arms) returned rc=1 in ~3s each with `ModuleNotFoundError: tac.substrates.pretrained_driving_prior.inflate` because the DP1 trainer's `_write_runtime` correctly invoked `shutil.copy2` to vendor 8 module bodies (architecture.py / archive.py / codebook.py / inflate.py / prior_application.py / procedural_codebook_inflate.py / seed_derived_codebook.py / _shared/inflate_runtime.py) into `output/submission/src/tac/.../`, but the harvester silently DROPPED all 8 via `artifact_mtime_floor = time.time() - 5.0` filter. Root cause: `shutil.copy2` preserves source mtime; source files at `/tmp/pact/src/tac/...` carry old local-repo mtimes (days/weeks) propagated through Modal's `copytree(symlinks=True)` mount staging. `write_text` / `write_bytes` (used for `__init__.py` stubs + top-level inflate.py string emission) produce current-mtime files that DID pass the floor, while `shutil.copy2` bodies did not. The mtime_floor's purpose is to filter STALE pre-existing artifacts from prior runs; it is WRONG to apply to `output/submission/` since that subtree is the trainer's atomic submission emission per Catalog #146 inflate runtime contract. Canonical fix bypasses the mtime_floor for `output/submission/` paths specifically. Per CLAUDE.md "Forbidden premature KILL without research exhaustion": this is IMPLEMENTATION-LEVEL fix, NOT paradigm change; DP1 substrate paradigm INTACT. Acceptance: (a) harvester loop contains canonical bypass tokens (`under_submission` variable + `parts[0] == "output"` + `parts[1] == "submission"` + `if not under_submission and st.st_mtime < artifact_mtime_floor`); (b) file-level waiver `# CATALOG_361_HARVESTER_FILTER_WAIVED:<rationale>` in first 80 lines with non-placeholder rationale (≥4 chars; placeholder `<rationale>` / `<reason>` literals rejected per Catalog #287 sister discipline so the gate's docstring example cannot self-waive). **Sister-extinction architecture**: companion defense-in-depth `tac.substrates._shared.trainer_skeleton.vendor_module_with_fresh_mtime` (canonical helper combining `shutil.copy2` + `os.utime(dst, None)`) makes substrate trainers robust against this filter even WITHOUT the META-layer fix; substrate trainers SHOULD route through the canonical helper instead of bare `shutil.copy2`. DP1 trainer's `_write_runtime` migrated to canonical helper in same commit batch. Sister of Catalog #166 (Modal worker source-parity ledger; same META infrastructure surface) + Catalog #245 (canonical Modal call_id ledger; same 4-layer canonical pattern) + Catalog #295 (submission inflate runtime self-containment at `submissions/*/inflate.py` PERMANENT surface; #361 is the `experiments/results/**/output/submission/` trainer-emitted DERIVED surface sister — different file glob, same self-containment invariant) + Catalog #146 (inflate runtime contract) + Catalog #339 (silent-no-spawn extinction; same META self-protection pattern at the dispatch-registration surface) + Catalog #176 (META-meta: STRICT callsites have CLAUDE.md row — THIS entry satisfies that META gate) + Catalog #185 (META-meta-meta: Live count: 0 verified empirically) + Catalog #287 (placeholder-rationale rejection) + Catalog #270 (umbrella dispatch optimization protocol; Tier 2 hardware-correctness sub-class at the META harvester surface). **Catalog #299 quota brake decision**: NEW gate (NOT scope extension of Catalog #295) because #295's surface is `submissions/*/inflate.py` (permanent PYTHONPATH self-containment) while #361's surface is `experiments/modal_train_lane.py::run_lane_inner` artifact harvester filter logic (META infrastructure mtime semantics) — different abstraction layers, different bug classes, different waiver mechanisms; sister-extinction architecture preferred over scope-stretch per #299 "stop and consolidate" pause discipline (current count 361 well under 400 quota; gate adds focused META protection rather than diluting #295's contract). STRICT-from-byte-one — live count at landing: 0 (META-layer fix + canonical helper land in same commit batch). 20 dedicated tests in `src/tac/tests/test_check_361_modal_artifact_filter_preserves_submission_dir.py` covering live-repo regression / synthetic regression no-bypass flagged / synthetic partial-bypass flagged / canonical bypass accepted / waiver-with-rationale accepted / placeholder + `<reason>` + empty + short rationales rejected / missing-target silent / string repo_root accepted / verbose output / vendor_module_with_fresh_mtime basic + creates-parent-dirs + end-to-end mtime_floor-filter-pass PV / orchestrator wire-in strict=True regression / Catalog #185 sister-callable regression. Plus 99 existing tests pass (25 DP1 dispatch_ready + 43 trainer_skeleton + 11 modal_train_lane_hardening + 20 new Catalog #361). **6-hook wire-in declaration** per Catalog #125: hook #1 sensitivity-map = N/A (defensive validator gate); hook #2 Pareto constraint = N/A; hook #3 bit-allocator = N/A; hook #4 cathedral autopilot dispatch = **ACTIVE** (prevents future Modal dispatcher regressions from silently dropping vendored submission_dir bodies and producing phantom rc=0 dispatches that crash inflate ModuleNotFoundError); hook #5 continual-learning posterior = N/A; hook #6 probe-disambiguator = **ACTIVE** (the canonical `under_submission` guard IS the disambiguator between stale-prior-run-artifact filtering vs trainer-emitted-submission-packet preservation).
